TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种针对视觉语言模型（VLM）的量化框架，使其能够在资源受限的移动设备上高效部署。该方法利用模型自身生成训练数据（无需访问原始训练流程），并结合一种新颖的2.7比特/参数格式，在Arm CPU上实现高效执行。作者将Llama 3.2 11B Vision Instruct模型压缩至3.7 GB（8位激活），在标准视觉问答任务上保持了强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在移动设备上部署面临显著的显存和计算资源挑战，需要一种无需访问原始训练数据即可实现的量化方案，同时支持移动端硬件（如Arm CPU）的高效推理。

Method: 采用"自生成数据"的量化管线（利用模型自身生成训练数据），结合新颖的2.7比特/参数量化格式，激活值保持8位精度，针对Arm CPU进行优化以实现高效执行。

Result: 将Llama 3.2 11B Vision Instruct模型压缩至3.7 GB（约2.7比特/参数），在标准视觉问答任务上保持较强性能。

Conclusion: 该框架证明了在无需原始训练数据的前提下，通过自生成数据和低比特量化格式，可以大幅度压缩VLM模型并在移动端硬件上高效部署，同时保持可接受的性能水平。

Abstract: Deploying vision-language models (VLMs) on mobile devices is challenging due to their significant memory and compute requirements. We present a framework for quantizing VLMs for efficient inference on resource-constrained hardware. Our approach combines a quantization pipeline that uses the model itself to generate training data and does not require access to the training setup, with a novel 2.7-bit-per-parameter format supporting efficient execution on Arm CPUs. We validate our approach by compressing the Llama 3.2 11B Vision Instruct model to 3.7 GB with 8-bit activations, preserving strong performance on a set of standard visual question answering tasks.

</details>


### [69] [Stream3Dv2: Geometric-Semantic Fusion Enhanced Streaming Zero-Shot 3D Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21136)
*Jie Xu,Na Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Stream3Dv2是一个无需训练的流式3D感知框架，通过嵌套局部-历史架构处理流式RGB-D输入，结合几何-语义融合机制和流形距离点云细化策略，实现稳健的开词汇零样本3D场景分割与检测，并可与LLM智能体集成实现语言驱动的3D场景理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉基础模型的开放词汇零样本3D场景理解方法虽然在替代数据密集型监督方法上具有潜力，但在实际部署中面临两大关键限制：无法高效处理流式RGB-D输入，以及对噪声2D分割掩码的固有脆弱性。论文旨在解决这两个瓶颈，实现鲁棒的流式3D感知。

Method: 提出Stream3Dv2框架，核心包含三部分：(1) 嵌套局部到历史的架构，用于捕捉多视图一致性同时降低计算开销以支持实时响应；(2) 几何-语义融合机制，通过显式利用语义引导并将3D分割形式化为点-集合合并与划分问题，以解决几何噪声和语义歧义；(3) 基于流形距离的点云细化策略，利用局部流形图进行点到流形优化以缓解欧氏距离度量导致的边界划分失败，并通过几何包围盒动态激活和更新历史实例以实现流形到流形的快速细化。

Result: 在公开数据集上的大量实验表明，Stream3Dv2在基础开放词汇流式3D分割和检测任务中持续优于现有基线。此外，与LLM智能体集成可实现先进的语言驱动3D场景理解，展示了其在开放世界具身智能方面的潜力。

Conclusion: Stream3Dv2通过训练免费的架构设计和多重机制创新，有效解决了流式3D感知中的计算效率和噪声鲁棒性问题，为开放词汇3D场景理解提供了新的解决方案，并在开放世界具身智能应用中展现出广阔前景。

Abstract: Recently, open-vocabulary zero-shot 3D scene understanding using vision foundation models has emerged as a promising alternative to data-intensive supervised methods. However, deploying these models in real-world scenarios is severely hindered by their inability to efficiently handle streaming RGB-D inputs and their inherent vulnerability to noise 2D segmentation masks. To address these critical limitations, we propose Stream3Dv2, a novel training-free framework designed for robust streaming 3D perception. Stream3Dv2 processes sequential data through an original nested local-to-historical architecture, capturing multi-view consistency while circumventing the high computational overhead so as to support timely responses. At its core, we introduce a comprehensive geometric-semantic fusion mechanism that resolves geometric noise and semantic ambiguity by explicitly utilizing semantic guidance and formulating 3D segmentation as solving point-and-set merging and partitioning problems. Furthermore, we present an innovative manifold-distance-based point cloud refinement strategy. This approach leverages local manifold graphs for point-to-manifold optimization that mitigates the boundary delineation failures caused by Euclidean-distance metrics, and employs geometric bounding boxes to dynamically activate and update historical instances for achieving rapid manifold-to-manifold refinement. Extensive experiments on public datasets demonstrate that Stream3Dv2 consistently outperforms existing baselines in foundational open-vocabulary streaming 3D segmentation and detection. Finally, we show that integrating our framework with an LLM-based agent enables advanced language-driven 3D scene understanding, underscoring its potential for open-world embodied intelligence. Code will be updated at https://github.com/SubmissionsIn/Stream3D.

</details>


### [70] [A Modular Agent for Reliable and Auditable Spatial Relation Verification in CT Scans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21140)
*Simon Vincent Abel,Heiko Hillenhagen,Michael Götz,Timo Ropinski,Ayhan Can Erdur,Daniel Santak Wolf*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种模块化医学影像智能体，用于轴向CT切片中的二元空间关系验证。系统将任务分解为语言解析、解剖定位和确定性几何验证三个阶段，而非端到端直接预测。在MIRP空间QA基准上达到94.1%准确率和94.2% F1，比直接使用Qwen2-VL提示高出42.5个百分点，同时保持可解释的中间表示和可审计的推理步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学视觉语言模型在受控空间推理方面表现薄弱，难以可靠地将空间关系锚定到图像证据上。由于放射学推理依赖于理解解剖结构和发现的相对位置，这种空间能力缺陷对诊断准确性构成风险，因此需要一个更可靠的空间理解系统。

Method: 模块化医学影像智能体将空间关系验证任务分解为三个显式阶段：(1) 语言解析：将自然语言查询转换为结构化关系元组；(2) 解剖定位：使用基于YOLO的检测器对查询器官进行定位；(3) 确定性几何验证：利用对象中心位置通过确定性几何规则计算最终空间决策。

Result: 在MIRP空间QA基准的held-out测试集上，最佳混合配置达到94.1%准确率和94.2% F1分数，在准确率上比直接Qwen2-VL提示高出42.5个百分点，同时保留了可解释的中间表示和可审计的推理阶段。

Conclusion: 显式的模块化空间验证可以作为面向报告生成的未来医学影像智能体的有前景的构建模块，为空间推理提供了比端到端VLM更可靠且更可审计的替代方案。

Abstract: Reliable spatial understanding is an important prerequisite for future medical vision-language systems that aim to support radiological report generation and structured image understanding. While modern vision-language models (VLMs) show promising performance on many medical imaging tasks, recent evidence suggests they remain weak in controlled spatial reasoning and often fail to reliably ground spatial relations in image evidence. Given that radiological reasoning hinges on understanding the relative positions of anatomical structures and findings, this spatial weakness poses risks to diagnostic accuracy. We present a modular medical imaging agent for binary spatial relation verification in axial CT slices. Instead of directly predicting spatial answers end-to-end, the system decomposes the task into explicit stages: language parsing, anatomical localization, and deterministic geometric verification. Natural-language queries are converted into structured relation tuples, queried organs are localized with a YOLO-based detector, and the final spatial decision is computed from object centers using deterministic geometric rules. We evaluate the approach on the held-out MIRP spatial QA benchmark and compare it against representative end-to-end VLM baselines. The best-performing hybrid configuration reaches 94.1% accuracy and 94.2% F1, outperforming direct Qwen2-VL prompting by 42.5 percentage points in accuracy, while preserving interpretable intermediate representations and auditable reasoning stages. The results suggest that explicit modular spatial verification can serve as a promising building block for future report-oriented medical imaging agents.

</details>


### [71] [Human-JEPA: A Human-Centric Vision Model that Perceives and Anticipates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21160)
*Hui Wei,Licai Sun,Guoying Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Human-JEPA是一个以人为中心的视觉模型，通过"锚定预测"（anchored forecasting）在视频上训练：将密集目标锚定在初始化副本上防止密集感知崩塌，并用纯过去到未来的分割替代块掩码，避免动作和重识别性能下降。在冻结探针评估下，它以2.7倍更少的参数在姿态估计和人重识别上领先像素锚定专家，其预测头首次不损害未来预测能力，一个模型同时服务当下感知与未来预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的以人为中心的视觉模型仅基于静态图像预训练，在稠密感知上达到最先进水平，但无法处理动态和未来预测。研究者希望构建一个既能感知当下又能预测未来的统一人体视觉模型。

Method: 提出Human-JEPA，在视频上进行训练，核心创新包括：(1) 锚定预测：将稠密预测目标固定在初始化的冻结副本上，防止稠密感知能力发生静默崩塌；(2) 用纯过去到未来的时间分割替代块掩码，避免对动作识别和重识别带来性能惩罚。

Result: 在冻结探针评估下，Human-JEPA在姿态估计和人重识别任务上超越像素锚定专家，且参数减少2.7倍；在高层级稠密解析上略有让步；其发布的预测头是首个不降低未来预测性能的预测头。

Conclusion: 一个安全适配的单一模型能够同时服务于"理解人类"的两个方面——当下的稠密感知与未来的动作预测，验证了通过视频预训练和锚定预测策略可以统一静态感知与动态预测能力。

Abstract: Machines that understand humans should perceive the present and anticipate the future. Existing human-centric vision model are pretrained on human images, set the state of the art in static dense perception, so motion and anticipation are out of reach. Here we present Human-JEPA, a human-centric vision model trained on video by anchored forecasting: dense targets are pinned to a frozen copy of the initialization, preventing a silent collapse of dense perception, and block masks are replaced by a pure past-to-future split, avoiding a five-point action tax and a seventeen-point re-identification collapse. Under frozen probes, Human-JEPA leads the pixel-anchored specialists on pose and person re-identification at 2.7 times fewer parameters, conceding high-resolution dense parsing, and its released predictor head is the first that does not degrade anticipation. A single safely adapted model thus serves both halves of understanding humans.

</details>


### [72] [Is Visual Prompting All You Need? Studying VLM Spatial Reasoning under Progressive Visual Scaffolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21170)
*Lars Benedikt Kaesberg,Tianyu Yang,Florian Valentin Wunderlich,Terry Ruas,Jan Philip Wahle,Daniel Kurzawe,Bela Gipp*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过SPaRC基准（网格视觉空间规划任务），引入轻量级输入侧脚手架（保持视觉模态但使空间结构更易获取），证明视觉呈现方式在底层推理问题不变的情况下会显著影响VLM的表现和失败模式，最高提升34.0个百分点准确率，并与GRPO训练互补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究指出VLM的失败常源于视觉接地与下游推理的交互，但视觉呈现方式本身如何影响模型性能尚不清楚。本文旨在厘清：在推理问题不变的情况下，视觉呈现是否是决定基准测量内容（接地感知、下游推理或两者混合）的关键因素。

Method: 在SPaRC（基于网格的视觉空间规划基准）上引入轻量级输入侧脚手架，保持视觉模态的同时让空间结构更易被模型获取；结合GRPO（组相对策略优化）训练；通过端到端任务求解和目标检测两个层面的分析，拆解接地与推理误差来源。

Result: 脚手架在原始视觉设置基础上提升任务准确率最高达34.0个百分点；与GRPO训练互补可再提升4.6个百分点（而原始视觉输入下GRPO增益接近零）；增益主要来自接地相关错误的减少，规则推理仍然相对困难。

Conclusion: 视觉呈现是决定VLM基准测量本质的核心因素——它决定基准评估的是接地感知、下游推理还是两者的混合。这提示在设计VLM基准和训练策略时，视觉呈现方式应当被视为关键可控变量。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have advanced rapidly in multimodal reasoning, yet recent work shows that their failures often reflect an interaction between visual grounding and downstream reasoning. What remains less clear is how the visual presentation of a task shapes model performance and failure modes when the underlying reasoning problem is unchanged. We study this question in SPaRC, a benchmark for grid-based visual spatial planning, by introducing lightweight input-side scaffolds that preserve the visual modality while making spatial structure more accessible. Across multiple VLMs, these scaffolds improve task accuracy over the original visual setting by up to 34.0 percentage points and further complement GRPO-based training, yielding up to 4.6 additional accuracy points compared with near-zero gains on the original visual input. Analyses on both end-to-end task solving and object detection show that these gains are closely tied to reductions in grounding-related errors, while rule reasoning remains comparatively challenging. We find that visual presentation is a central factor that determines whether VLM benchmarks measure grounded perception, downstream reasoning, or a mixture of both.

</details>


### [73] [Towards Investigating Residual Hearing Loss: Quantification of Fibrosis in a Novel Cochlear OCT Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21189)
*Julia Dietlmeier,Benjamin Greenberg,Wenxuan He,Teresa Wilson,Rubing Xing,Jordan Hill,Adrienne Fettig,Madeline Otto,Teyhana Rounsavill,Lina A. J. Reiss,Jingang Yi,Noel E. O'Connor,George W. S. Burwood*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次将计算机视觉技术应用于植入人工耳蜗（CI）的耳蜗纤维化OCT图像数据集，通过改进UNET架构（2D-OCT-UNET）实现了对耳蜗纤维化的高精度语义分割，为混合人工耳蜗（EAS）患者纤维化负担的客观评估提供了可靠工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合人工耳蜗（EAS）结合残余低频听力和电刺激，但植入物引发的耳蜗内纤维化会损害残余听力并降低EAS疗效。因此需要客观、高分辨率且可重复的方法来评估啮齿动物模型中的纤维化负担，以推进减少纤维化、改善患者预后的转化研究目标。

Method: 研究者构建了慢性植入豚鼠耳的OCT图像数据集，并人工标注了耳蜗纤维化等区域。随后对比多种最先进的语义分割模型，并对经典UNET架构进行修改（即2D-OCT-UNET），在放大后的OCT输入分辨率上运行以提升分割性能。

Result: 实验表明，改进的2D-OCT-UNET模型在所有对比的语义分割模型中表现最佳，能够可靠地计算耳蜗纤维化负担，验证了深度学习在该医学影像分析任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: 这是首次成功将计算机视觉技术应用于植入耳蜗的OCT纤维化数据集。该深度学习模型使耳蜗纤维化负担的计算变得可靠可行，数据集和代码已开源发布。

Abstract: Objective: Cochlear implants (CIs) are bionic prostheses that restores hearing via electrical stimulation of the auditory nerve. Hybrid CIs, which use electroacoustic stimulation (EAS), combine residual low-frequency acoustic hearing with CI electrical stimulation. Intracochlear fibrosis, which forms in response to the presence of the implant, may impede residual hearing function and gradually reduce the efficacy of EAS. It is therefore a translational objective to study the formation of cochlear fibrosis in rodents, with the goal of reducing fibrotic burden and improving outcomes for CI patients. Methods: We generate and annotate a novel dataset of optical coherence tomography (OCT) images from chronically implanted guinea pigs as part of an ongoing study focused on implant induced fibrosis. Objectively assessing fibrotic burden in this model, with high resolution and repeatability, presents an obvious use case for computer vision methods. Results: We present the results of several state-of-the-art semantic segmentation models and compare their efficacy for identifying cochlear fibrosis and other relevant annotations, using a new library of manually segmented OCT images. Conclusions: We find that the best performance is achieved by using a modified version of the well-known UNET architecture (which we term 2D-OCT-UNET) that operates on the upscaled OCT input resolution. Significance: For the first time, we have successfully applied computer vision techniques to an OCT dataset of implanted cochleae with fibrosis. Using this deep learning model, the cochlear fibrotic burden calculation can be reliably carried out as we verify in our experimental section. The dataset and the project code are available at: https://github.com/juliadietlmeier/CF-OCT-segmentation

</details>


### [74] [ES-VP : Energy-Shaped Dynamic Visual Prompting for Efficient Model Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21194)
*Can Jin,Ying Li,Jingchen Sun,Hongwu Peng,Jiahui Zhao,Yang Zhou,Lei Li,Dimitris N. Metaxas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出能量形状视觉提示（ES-VP）方法，通过低秩初始化和能量引导动态适应生成图像特定的提示，在参数效率与性能之间取得更好平衡，相比单提示方法用更少参数实现更优性能，在五个架构十五个数据集上超越现有SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉提示方法在灵活性与效率之间存在权衡：固定提示忽略图像个性特征，而辅助网络生成多样化提示虽提升性能但增加参数使用量并可能过拟合，且辅助网络配合预训练模型固有偏见限制可扩展性与泛化能力。

Method: ES-VP方法包含两个核心组件：1）低秩初始化用于生成图像特定提示；2）能量引导的动态自适应机制。该方法直接利用预训练模型本身进行自适应提示生成，无需辅助网络，从而保证参数效率和泛化能力。

Result: 在五个架构十五个数据集上的大量实验表明，ES-VP持续优于现有的单提示和多样提示SOTA方法。例如在CLIP架构的四个数据集上，ES-VP相比SOTA方法DAM-VP平均准确率提升2.6%，同时使用的VP参数减少590倍。

Conclusion: ES-VP为高效且可泛化的模型适配建立了新的基准，证明通过巧妙的设计可以在极低的参数开销下获得优于现有方法的性能。

Abstract: Visual prompting (VP) has emerged as a parameter-efficient method for adapting pre-trained models to downstream tasks. However, existing approaches encounter a trade-off between flexibility and efficiency. Some methods apply a fixed prompt to all images, ignoring individual image characteristics, while others introduce auxiliary networks to generate diverse prompts. Although the latter can improve performance, it also significantly increases parameter usage and the potential for overfitting to specific datasets. Furthermore, the auxiliary networks, combined with inherent biases in pre-trained models, limit scalability and generalization. In this paper, we propose Energy-Shaped Visual Prompting (ES-VP), a novel approach that generates image-specific prompts using low-rank initialization and energy-guided dynamic adaptation, achieving superior performance with fewer parameters compared to single-prompt methods. ES-VP directly utilizes the pre-trained model for adaptive prompt generation, ensuring both parameter efficiency and improved generalization. Extensive experiments conducted on five architectures across fifteen datasets demonstrate that ES-VP consistently outperforms current state-of-the-art (SOTA) single and diverse VP methods. For instance, using the CLIP architecture across four datasets, ES-VP outperforms the SOTA method DAM-VP by an average of 2.6\% in accuracy while utilizing 590$\times$ fewer VP parameters, thereby establishing a new benchmark for efficient and generalizable model adaptation.

</details>


### [75] [Anchoring Instruction Outside Mask: Exact Reference Caching for Efficient In-Context Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21229)
*Yangshuai Liu,Zheming Li,Jiaao Li,Kang He,Ziliang Lai,Zhitai Liu,Chengru Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种超越掩码（beyond-mask）的Omnimodal生成方法，通过静态文本锚点连接指令与参考分支，在保留KV缓存复用（加速推理）的同时实现了全注意力质量的生成效果，并通过教师强制的速度蒸馏加在线策略蒸馏来恢复架构转换带来的质量损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Omnimodal生成中，多参考图像的in-context条件化会导致数千个token，计算成本随参考数量快速增长。现有的结构化稀疏注意力方法虽然实现了KV复用加速，但阻碍了视觉参考与文本指令的交互，严重损害了指令跟随和多参考编辑的参考保真度。

Method: 1) 联合重新设计token序列和注意力掩码，引入静态文本锚点连接指令与参考分支；2) 保留精确的KV复用无需增加参数；3) 通过教师强制的速度蒸馏，再经过短期的在线策略蒸馏阶段（教师监督学生访问的状态）来恢复生成质量。

Result: 在三个图像编辑基准上与全注意力生成质量相当。使用五张参考图像时，完整40步去噪过程加速3.92倍，静态文本锚点几乎无运行时开销；在缩放实验中，十张参考时加速达5.47倍。

Conclusion: 该工作是首次将在线策略蒸馏用于扩散模型的架构恢复，成功解决了稀疏注意力中参考与文本指令交互受限与高效KV复用之间的矛盾，在不增加参数的前提下兼顾了质量与效率。

Abstract: Omnimodal generation is central to a wide range of content creation and editing applications. In-context conditioning is essential to this paradigm. It allows diffusion transformers to process text instructions and visual references in a shared attention sequence. However, each reference image introduces thousands of tokens. Computation therefore grows rapidly with the number of references. Existing methods reduce computation through structured sparse attention, which limits interactions between reference and target tokens. This structure also makes the reference K and V independent of the denoising target, allowing them to be computed once and reused across steps. However, it blocks visual references from attending to the text instruction. This substantially degrades instruction following and reference fidelity in multi-reference editing. To resolve this conflict, we jointly redesign the token sequence and attention mask. Our beyond-mask design uses static text anchors to connect the instruction to the reference branch. It preserves exact K and V reuse without adding parameters. However, this direct architectural conversion degrades generation quality. We recover the lost performance through teacher-forced velocity distillation, followed by a short on-policy stage in which the teacher supervises student-visited states. To our knowledge, this is the first use of on-policy distillation for architectural recovery in diffusion models. Across three image-editing benchmarks, our method matches full-attention generation quality. With five reference images, it accelerates the complete 40-step denoising process by 3.92x, while static text anchors introduce negligible runtime overhead; the speedup reaches 5.47x at ten references in our scaling study.

</details>


### [76] [A VLM Answer Is Not an Anomaly Score: Rank Compression in Training-Free Video Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21244)
*Inpyo Song,Jangwon Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探讨了视觉语言模型（VLM）在无监督视频异常检测（VAD）中的应用，核心发现是"答案接口"（answer interface）——包括答案尺度和读出规则——是检测器的重要组成部分而非格式化细节。概率读出（probability readout）利用完整答案分布，在所有测试的VLM、答案尺度、基准和指标组合下均优于仅使用最可能答案的生成读出（generated readout），平均提升5-13个点。作者将生成读出的性能缺陷归因于"生成答案排名压缩"，即不同答案分布的片段可能获得相同分数从而丢失相对顺序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VAD基准要求为每个视频片段提供标量异常分数并用AUROC或AP评估排名，但VLM-based检测器需要定义答案接口（答案尺度和读出规则）来将模型输出转换为分数。由于这个接口会影响评估排名，它应被视为检测器的一部分，值得系统研究。

Method: 作者系统比较了两种读出规则：生成读出（仅使用最可能答案的概率）和概率读出（使用所有可接受答案的完整分布）。在四种7-8B VLM上，对不同答案尺度、基准和指标组合进行了全面实验，并分析了各种解码策略、提示措辞和联合评分-解释提示条件下的鲁棒性。

Result: 概率读出在所有测试条件下均优于生成读出，平均增益从5到13点不等。即使答案尺度允许91个答案，生成读出仅产生4-18个不同分数，而概率读出保留了更精细的分数分辨率。优势在每种测试的解码策略、提示措辞和联合评分-解释提示下均持续存在。

Conclusion: 答案接口是VLM-based VAD中具有实质影响的组件，应当被明确指定和评估。研究者报告结果时应透明化其答案接口选择，以实现公平和可重复的比较。

Abstract: Vision-language models enable training-free video anomaly detection by answering questions about video segments. VAD benchmarks, however, require a scalar anomaly score for each segment and evaluate the resulting ranking using the AUROC or AP. A VLM-based detector should therefore define an answer interface: the answer scale specifies the admissible answers, and the readout rule maps the model's output distribution to a score. Because this interface can change the evaluated ranking, it is part of the detector rather than a formatting detail. The generated readout uses only the most likely answer, whereas the probability readout uses the full distribution over admissible answers. Across four 7-8B VLMs, the probability readout outperforms the generated readout for every tested combination of answer scale, benchmark, and metric, with average gains ranging from 5 to 13 points across the four benchmark-metric pairs. The gap arises because the generated readout keeps only one answer value per segment, so segment with different answer distributions can receive the same score and lose their relative order. We call this loss of relative order generated-answer rank compression. Even when the answer scale allows 91 answers, the generated readout produces only 4-18 distinct scores, whereas the probability readout retains substantially finer score resolution. The advantage persists under every decoding strategy, prompt wording, and joint scoring-explanation prompt we test. The answer interface is therefore a consequential component of VLM-based VAD and should be explicitly specified and evaluated.

</details>


### [77] [On the Transferability of Agricultural Weed Detection Under Cross-Field Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21254)
*Nikhilesh Prabhakar,Pranuthi Tenali,Wilfredo Abudeye Fernandez,Shekhar Borah,Athresh Karanam,Erik Blasch,Prabha Sundaravadivel,Sriraam Natarajan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了农业杂草检测模型在不同作物和田间场景下的跨数据集泛化性能。作者收集了棉花田的无人机杂草检测数据集，与已有的大豆数据集进行跨作物迁移实验，对比了无监督域适应目标检测（DAOD）和源域预训练加少样本微调两种策略。结果发现，仅需25个带标签目标样本的少样本微调性能优于无监督DAOD，表明源域选择加少量目标监督比算法复杂性更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有杂草检测模型大多在单一作物和田间评估，缺乏跨作物或跨田间泛化的证据。实际部署中，农民需要在新作物或新田地使用模型，如果模型无法泛化则需重新标注每个部署场景，成本高昂。因此需要系统研究跨数据集杂草检测性能退化的情况，并找出能恢复性能的建模策略。

Method: 1) 收集并标注了棉花田的无人机杂草检测新数据集，与已有的大豆数据集一起使用（两者采集协议相似）；2) 评估跨作物迁移性能，比较两种迁移策略：无监督域适应目标检测（DAOD）与源域预训练后目标域少样本微调；3) 分析目标域标注预算从零到全量数据的范围，刻画适应策略与标注工作量之间的权衡。

Result: 在跨作物对比中，少样本微调仅使用25个带标签目标示例就优于无监督DAOD方法。这表明在杂草检测的跨作物迁移场景下，源域选择配合适度的目标域监督比复杂的算法适应性设计更能恢复检测性能。

Conclusion: 对于农业杂草检测的跨作物部署，采用域邻近的源数据集进行预训练，再以少量（如25个）目标域标注样本进行微调，是比无监督域适应更实用有效且经济的策略，可减少每个新部署田地的重新标注需求。

Abstract: Accurate agricultural weed detection in real-world field conditions is essential for precision agriculture, enabling targeted intervention and reducing yield loss. Recent work has reported strong detection performance from UAV-based imagery across a range of crops, yet existing approaches evaluate within a single crop and field, leaving practitioners with little evidence that a model trained on one crop will generalize to a new field or crop type. In this work, we characterize where cross-dataset weed-localization performance degrades and which modeling choices recover it, reducing the need to relabel every new deployment field. We introduce a newly collected and annotated UAV image dataset for agricultural weed detection in cotton fields and use it alongside an existing soybean dataset collected under a similar protocol. Using these datasets, we evaluate the performance of several strategies for transferring a detector trained on one crop to another, comparing unsupervised domain adaptive object detection (DAOD) against pretraining on a domain-adjacent source dataset followed by few-shot fine-tuning on the target dataset. Our analysis spans target-domain label budgets from zero to the full target dataset, characterizing the trade-off between adaptation strategy and annotation effort. We find that few-shot fine-tuning with as few as 25 labeled target examples outperforms unsupervised DAOD in our cross-crop comparison, suggesting that source domain selection combined with modest target supervision is more productive than algorithmic sophistication in adaptation.

</details>


### [78] [WildFin: An In-the-Wild Dataset for Fish Behavioral Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21281)
*Abigail G. Grassick,Jerome Tze-Hou Hsu,Ethan Lin,Ziang Liu,Max Whitton,Madelyn Hair,Liam Gutierrez,Haozheng Yu,Kristin Branson,Vivek Jayaraman,Michael A. Gil,Andrew M. Hein,Jennifer J. Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了WildFin，一个由生态学家收集和标注的鱼类行为识别基准数据集，涵盖静态相机监测鱼群和动态潜水员追踪个体两种真实场景，包含1350小时野外工作和600小时专家标注产生的9小时行为数据及超200万帧级标签。通过基准测试现代视觉基础模型，揭示了当前模型能力与真实水下行为分析需求之间的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野外生态视频数据激增，但专家标注成本高昂是主要瓶颈；现有计算机视觉模型在复杂海洋环境中部署时频繁失败，缺乏一个专门用于表征和量化这些失败的基准数据集。

Method: 构建WildFin基准数据集：由生态学家参与收集和标注，覆盖静态相机监测鱼群和动态潜水员追踪个体两种真实世界范式；基于该数据集基准测试现代视觉基础模型，并量化静态架构与时空架构之间的权衡。

Result: 创建了包含超过200万帧级标签、9小时行为数据的WildFin数据集，并通过对现代视觉基础模型的基准测试，量化了静态与时空架构的表现差异，揭示了当前模型能力与真实水下行为分析需求之间的显著差距。

Conclusion: WildFin作为首个面向鱼类行为识别的生态学驱动基准，明确了当前视觉模型在水下行为分析中的不足，为未来水上视觉模型的研究提供了基准和方向。

Abstract: Recent advances in field technology have led to a massive influx of in-the-wild video data for ecological science. The primary bottleneck in leveraging this data is the high cost of expert annotation. While computer vision offers a potential solution, current models frequently fail when deployed in complex marine environments. To characterize these failures, we introduce WildFin, a novel benchmark for fish behavior recognition collected and annotated by ecologists.WildFin spans two critical real-world paradigms: stationary cameras monitoring groups of fish and dynamic divers following individual subjects. The dataset represents a massive curation effort, involving 1,350 hours of fieldwork and 600 hours of expert annotation to produce 9 hours of behavioral data with over 2 million frame-by-frame labels. We benchmark modern vision foundation models and quantify tradeoffs between static and spatiotemporal architectures, revealing the substantial gap that remains between current model capabilities and the demands of real-world underwater behavioral analysis. Project website: https://team-wildfin.github.io/.

</details>


### [79] [Difficulty-Calibrated Interpolation Paths for Conditional Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21286)
*Airin Akter Tania,Md Raihan Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Difficulty-Calibrated Flow Matching (DCFM)，该方法通过一次短暂的线性路径预训练获取沿路径的逐时间损失（即难度分布），然后将插值调度设置为该难度分布的分位数函数，使训练轨迹在速度最难学习的区域停留更久。该方法仅有单个超参数，不改变训练目标，可与classifier-free guidance兼容，开销仅增加约2%。实验表明在CIFAR-10、MNIST、Fashion-MNIST上使用相同的紧凑U-Net，校准路径在CIFAR-10全采样预算下取得最佳FID，并在大批量、少更新的计算紧缺场景下显著优于所有固定调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件流匹配（CFM）中，插值调度（schedule）显著影响收敛速度和样本质量，但现有方法总是预先固定调度，与数据和模型无关。作者通过观察发现，CFM的回归难度沿路径存在系统性变化，因此提出让调度自适应于模型和数据本身。

Method: 先用线性路径进行短暂的pilot run，记录每个时刻的损失以构建难度剖面（difficulty profile）；然后将插值调度设置为该难度分布的分位数函数，使得模型在速度最难学习的区域分配更多训练时间。该方法只有一个超参数，不改变训练目标，保持梯度等价性，并可与classifier-free guidance组合使用。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、MNIST、Fashion-MNIST上使用相同的紧凑U-Net进行受控实验，校准调度在CIFAR-10全采样预算下获得最佳FID，并在大批量、少更新的few-update场景中显著优于所有固定调度方案，而训练开销仅增加约2%。

Conclusion: 训练难度沿插值路径不均匀分布，通过让调度自适应于模型自身观测到的难度，可以在不改变训练目标的前提下提升样本质量，尤其在计算资源受限（大批量、少更新）的实际场景中收益最为明显。

Abstract: Conditional Flow Matching trains generative models by regressing a network onto the velocity of a prescribed noise-to-data interpolation path. The interpolation schedule that shapes this path is known to affect convergence and sample quality, yet it is invariably fixed in advance, independent of both the data and the model. We show that the regression difficulty of Conditional Flow Matching varies systematically along the path, and we propose Difficulty-Calibrated Flow Matching, which derives the schedule from the model itself: a short pilot run with the linear path records the per-time loss, and the schedule is set to the quantile function of this difficulty profile, so the trajectory lingers where the velocity is hardest to learn. The method has a single hyperparameter, leaves the training objective and its gradient equivalence intact, composes with classifier-free guidance, and adds about two percent training overhead. In controlled experiments on CIFAR-10, MNIST, and Fashion-MNIST with an identical compact U-Net, the calibrated path attains the best FID on CIFAR-10 at full sampling budget and clearly outperforms all fixed schedules in the large-batch, few-update regime, precisely the setting where compute is scarcest.

</details>


### [80] [When Adaptation Hurts: Connecting Representational Drift to OOD Failures in MedSAM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21300)
*Marko Haralović,Sounic Akkaraju,Carlo Baretta,Vasil Zapryanov,Alexia Briassouli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了MedSAM在医学图像分割中的泛化能力，探索了六种适应策略（包括LoRA、视觉提示调优VPT和全量微调）在域内和域外（OOD）数据集上的表现，发现全量微调效果最佳，编码器-only LoRA是参数高效的优选方案，并揭示远OOD性能下降与解码器表征漂移密切相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示式基础模型（如MedSAM）在医学图像分割中展现出良好泛化能力，但其性能高度依赖提示质量和模型对自定义数据集的适应程度。现有研究缺乏对MedSAM在不同适应策略和噪声提示条件下的系统性评估，特别是对OOD场景下的鲁棒性理解不足。

Method: 在ISIC 2018数据集上训练六种适应策略：全模型LoRA、编码器-only LoRA、浅层和深层视觉提示调优（VPT）、解码器-only和全量微调。在干净和逐渐增加噪声的提示条件下评估域内数据及三种OOD数据集（近OOD PH2、远OOD BUSI和CBIS-DDSM）。使用Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA)分析编码器和解码器表征的一致性。

Result: 适应提升了域内和近OOD性能但常降低远OOD性能；全量微调提供最佳权衡，编码器-only LoRA是最强的参数高效方案，优于标准LoRA和VPT；远OOD性能下降与解码器表征漂移强相关；随机0-100像素抖动提示能产生更鲁棒和性能更好的模型。

Conclusion: 鲁棒的MedSAM适应需要综合考虑提示噪声暴露、域偏移和表征保持三个方面。编码器-only LoRA通过适应视觉特征分布变化同时保留解码器通路，比标准LoRA提供更强的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Foundation models for medical image segmentation, like prompt-based MedSAM, generalize well across domains and modalities, often in zero or few-shot setups. However, their performance depends on the quality of prompts and the adaptation of the models to custom datasets. This work systematically examines how MedSAM generalizes across diverse medical imaging benchmarks, with six adaptation strategies: full-model and encoder-only LoRA, shallow and deep visual prompt tuning (VPT), and decoder-only and full fine-tuning. Models are trained on the International Skin Imaging Collaboration Challenge (ISIC 2018) dataset and evaluated under clean and increasingly noisy prompts on IN and Out-of-Distribution (OOD) datasets: close-OOD PH2 (dermoscopy), far-OOD BUSI (Breast Ultrasound Images Dataset) and CBIS-DDSM (Curated Breast Imaging Subset of the Digital Database for Screening Mammography). We show that adaptation improves performance on IN and close-OOD data but often reduces performance on far-OOD data. Full fine-tuning provides the best tradeoff, while encoder-only LoRA is the strongest parameter-efficient alternative, outperforming standard LoRA and VPT under far-OOD shifts. Using Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA), we show that far-OOD degradation is strongly associated with drift in decoder representations, whereas encoder similarity alone does not explain robustness. This suggests encoder-only LoRA provides stronger robustness than standard LoRA by adapting the encoder to distribution shift in visual features, while preserving the decoder pathway. We further show that random 0-100 pixel jitter on prompts produces more robust and better performing models. We thus conclude that robust MedSAM adaptation requires the combined consideration of prompt noise exposure, domain shift, and representation preservation. We release our code: https://github.com/ImSounic/medsam-vpt

</details>


### [81] [Re$^3$Cap: Retrieval-Guided Refinement for Image Captioning Enhancement via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21305)
*Haonan Jia,Shichao Dong,Zenghui Sun,Jiawen Zheng,Ziqi Miao,Gege Shi,Qiuyu Zhao,Jinsong Lan,Xiaoyong Zhu,Bo Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Re³Cap，一种基于多模态检索引导的推理策略，用于增强大视觉语言模型（LVLMs）的图像描述生成能力，无需额外标注即可通过检索引导识别并修正幻觉和遗漏，在多项基准上超越监督微调（SFT）和GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习（RL）在图像描述任务中虽已取得显著进展，但难以鼓励大视觉语言模型（LVLMs）探索新的推理策略，导致RL与监督微调（SFT）之间存在性能差距。作者认为多模态检索可以作为有效的推理信号来辅助描述精炼，从而弥补这一差距。

Method: 提出Re³Cap（Retrieval-Guided Refinement for Image Captioning）检索引导推理策略，通过两个组件实现：Caption Refinement Suggester（CRS）用于识别描述中的幻觉（hallucinations）和遗漏（omissions），以及Caption Quality Assessor（CQA）用于评估描述质量，两者协同引导模型生成更准确和详细描述，无需额外标注。

Result: 大量实验表明该方法在图像描述任务上优于现有方法，甚至超过监督微调（SFT）。特别是在COCO-LN500基准上，Re³Cap相对于GRPO在关系推理（relation reasoning）上平均提升8.64%。

Conclusion: 多模态检索可以作为有效的推理信号用于图像描述的引导精炼，Re³Cap通过检索引导的方式弥补了RL与SFT之间的性能差距，展现了无需额外标注即可提升图像描述质量的潜力。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has demonstrated significant gains in image captioning, yet it is still limited in encouraging Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) to explore novel reasoning strategies. This limitation leads to a performance gap between RL and Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT). In this paper, we argue that multi-modal retrieval can serve as an effective reasoning signal for caption refinement. Based on this insight, we present the Retrieval-Guided Refinement for Image Captioning (Re$^3$Cap), a retrieval-guided reasoning strategy that enhances image captioning without requiring additional annotations. Instantiated by Caption Refinement Suggester (CRS) and Caption Quality Assessor (CQA), this strategy identifies hallucinations and omissions in image captions, leading to more accurate and detailed descriptions. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superiority of our method in image captioning, even compared with Supervised Fine-Tuning. Especially, Re$^3$Cap outperforms GRPO with an average improvement of 8.64% in relation reasoning on the COCO-LN500 benchmark.

</details>


### [82] [OmniAssistBench: Assistant-style Interaction Benchmark for Omni-LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21360)
*Xianyun Sun,Chaoyou Fu,Zhengye Zhang,Feiyang Duan,Qingyuan Cao,Yonghui Niu,Sihang Yuan,Ge Zhang,Caifeng Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OmniAssistBench基准，用于评估全能多模态大语言模型作为实时视频助手的交互能力。通过逆向工程互联网视频构建数据集，要求模型沿预设路径引导用户完成任务，并发现现有模型（Gemini-3-Pro得66.4分，Qwen3-Omni-Instruct得51.2分）在视觉提示理解、多轮上下文保持及时机把握等方面仍有明显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统被动视频理解与交互式助手的本质区别在于：交互助手的不可预测响应会动态改变用户后续行为，静态离线数据集无法适应这种交互动态性。同时，同一用户目标可通过多种方式实现，导致交互路径发散，现有评估方法无法有效解决这一挑战。

Method: (1) 提出交互式视频助手评估基准OmniAssistBench；(2) 为模型提供源自源视频的预定义先验，要求其引导用户沿完全相同路线完成任务，解决交互路径发散问题；(3) 通过逆向工程现有互联网视频，推断逻辑用户目标并将视频分割为多轮片段以模拟连续交互；(4) 该数据集构建管线耗费超过1000专家工时。

Result: 商业模型Gemini-3-Pro得分66.4（满分100），开源模型Qwen3-Omni-Instruct得分51.2。尽管模型普遍理解用户输入，但经常给出错误或不完整的回答；在视觉提示（如手势）、多轮交互中的历史上下文保持、以及延迟响应直到目标事件出现等方面存在明显短板。

Conclusion: 当前全能多模态大语言模型距离成为可靠的实时交互式视频助手仍有较大提升空间，OmniAssistBench为该领域提供了可复现、可量化的评估框架。

Abstract: Recent omni-modal large language models (Omni-LLMs) show great potential as real-time video assistants, which continuously perceive environments and guide users to achieve specific goals. Unlike traditional passive video understanding, interactive assistants should actively combine visual states, user goals, and prior knowledge to provide effective help. Evaluating this is rather challenging, as the model's unpredictable response dynamically changes the user's subsequent actions, which static offline datasets cannot accommodate. To address this bottleneck, we introduce OmniAssistBench. To solve the issue of diverging interaction paths where the same user goal can be achieved through various methods, we provide models with predefined priors derived from the source video, requiring them to guide users along the exact same routes. Since real interaction videos are rare, we construct the dataset by reverse-engineering existing Internet videos. We deduce logical user goals and segment the videos into multi-turn clips to simulate continuous interactions. This rigorous pipeline required over 1000 expert person-hours to build the dataset. Results show that the proprietary Gemini-3-Pro reaches 66.4 out of the max point of 100, while the open-source Qwen3-Omni-Instruct achieves 51.2. Although current models generally understand user inputs, they frequently provide incorrect or incomplete answers. Specifically, they struggle with visual prompts (e.g., hand gestures), fail to maintain historical context during multi-turn interactions, and fail to delay response until the target event. Results indicate substantial room for improvement before models can become reliable assistants.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

### [83] [Humanoid Musical Robots as Experimental Interfaces for Music-Evoked Emotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20433)
*Vincent K. M. Cheung,Jia-Yeu Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新视角：将音乐人形机器人作为研究音乐诱发情感的实验界面。作者认为现有研究受限于预录音频刺激范式，无法捕捉真实音乐体验的多模态、具身和交互特性，而人形机器人可通过参数化控制、可重复性和声学/非声学变量的解耦重组，为未来音乐-情感实验提供方法论平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音乐诱发情感的研究依赖预录音频刺激，未能体现真实音乐体验中多模态、具身化和交互性的本质。技术发展使音乐人形机器人日益成熟，但其应用多局限于表演和人机交互研究，尚未被用作情感研究的实验工具。

Method: 基于音乐认知与情感理论，识别需要同时操控声学与非声学变量的机制；论证人形机器人具备参数化控制表演变量、跨试验可复现性，以及解耦并重组听觉、视觉与交互组件的能力；并以WAseda萨克斯机器人5号（WAS-5）为案例研究验证技术可行性。

Result: 以WAS-5为案例，演示了对声学与交互变量的可重复控制，验证了该视角的技术可行性，为未来音乐-情感实验奠定了前置条件。

Conclusion: 音乐人形机器人可作为方法论平台，推动未来对音乐诱发情感的可控实验研究，弥补现有范式在具身性、多模态与交互性方面的不足。

Abstract: Advances in technology have led to increasingly sophisticated musical humanoid robots. However, their use has largely been limited to performance and related research in human-robot interaction. In this position paper, we propose a novel perspective: musical humanoid robots as experimental interfaces for investigating music-evoked emotions. We argue that current research is constrained by paradigms relying on pre-recorded auditory stimuli, which fail to capture the multimodal, embodied, and interactive nature of real-world musical experience. Building on existing theories of music cognition and emotion, we identify mechanisms that require controlled manipulation of both acoustic and non-acoustic variables. We show that humanoid robots are well-suited as they enable parametric control of performance variables, reproducibility across trials, and the decoupling and recombination of auditory, visual, and interactive components. We illustrate the technical feasibility of this perspective through a case study of the WAseda Saxophonist Robot 5 (WAS-5), demonstrating reproducible control of acoustic and interaction variables that are prerequisites for future music-emotion experiments. Our work positions musical humanoid robots as a methodological platform that enables future controlled investigations of music-evoked emotions.

</details>


### [84] [EndoLIFT: Language-Disambiguated Latent-Conditioned Rectified Flow for Bidirectional Endoscopic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20478)
*Chi Kit Ng,Yidong Zhang,Lui Siu Hing,Jinsong Lin,Tianchun Wu,Ho Yin Chim,Zhiqing Tang,Tao Yang,Huxin Gao,Trevor Yeung,Raymond Shing-Yan Tang,Hongliang Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出EndoLIFT，一种用于双向内窥镜控制的视觉-语言-动作策略，通过显式语言意图条件化与轨迹潜在变量的整流流动作专家相结合，解决双向内窥镜控制中的"意图混叠"（intent aliasing）问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规胃肠内窥镜检查本质上是双向的：器械需前进到达目标解剖位置，之后回撤或反转进行检查，而外部提示可能要求提前反向。当请求的阶段在视觉场景变化之前改变时，几乎相同的观测可能需要相反轴向动作。这种模糊性（即意图混叠）会使策略难以区分何时前进或回撤。

Method: 提出EndoLIFT框架：包含(1)基于语言指令的显式意图条件化；(2)潜在条件化的整流流动作专家（rectified-flow action expert）。策略接收RGB图像、语言指令和先前动作状态，通过32维变分轨迹潜在变量随机条件化连续动作块生成。并通过受控的同观测指令交换实验验证语言如何选择轴向模式。

Result: 相比无潜在条件化的匹配模型，EndoLIFT导航方向准确率提升11.1个百分点，错误方向前进减少83%。保留82.8%的意图跟随准确率（44个未见语言变体）。闭环评估中，EndoLIFT在可见结肠幻影及未见肺和胃幻影上整体成功率提升30个百分点，并完成10/10离体猪气管试验。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明语言基于的意图选择与轨迹潜在变量对方向正确性和稳健回撤的贡献是分离的，两者结合可显著提升双向内窥镜控制的性能。

Abstract: Routine gastrointestinal endoscopy is intrinsically bidirectional: the instrument is advanced to reach target anatomy and later withdrawn or retroflexed for inspection, while an external cue may require earlier reversal. When the requested phase changes before the visual scene does, nearly identical observations can require opposite axial actions. We identify and formalize this ambiguity in bidirectional endoscopic control as intent aliasing. We propose EndoLIFT (Endoscopic Language-Instruction Flow with Trajectory Latents), a vision-language-action policy that combines explicit language-based intent conditioning with a latent-conditioned rectified-flow action expert. The policy receives RGB, a language instruction, and the previous-action state; a 32-D variational trajectory latent stochastically conditions continuous action-chunk generation. Controlled same-observation instruction swaps establish that language selects the axial mode, independently of whether the trajectory latent is present. Relative to the matched model without latent conditioning, EndoLIFT improves navigation-direction accuracy by 11.1 percentage points and reduces wrong-direction advance by 83\%. An architecture-controlled 1-bit mode-flag reference exhibits weaker canonical-anchor switching, while EndoLIFT retains 82.8\% intent-following accuracy across 44 held-out linguistic variants. In closed-loop evaluation, EndoLIFT improves overall success by 30 percentage points over EndoLIFT w/o VTL on both the seen colon phantom and the unseen lung and stomach phantoms, and completes 10/10 ex-vivo porcine-trachea trials. These results separate language-based intent selection from the trajectory latent's contribution to directional correctness and robust retraction.

</details>


### [85] [Koala Gripper: Co-designing Robotic Grippers and Data-Capture Devices for Scaling Dexterous Manipulation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20546)
*Amar Hajj-Ahmad,Zubin Kremer Guha,Tim Fofonoff,Zhi Ern Teoh,Ciarán T. O'Neill,Ben Thacher,Igor Fala,Vidullan Surendran,Murphy Wonsick,Peter Whitney,David Watkins*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种协同设计框架，同时开发数据采集设备和机器人执行设备。基于此框架设计了Koala Gripper系统，包括一个数据采集设备和机器人夹爪平台。该系统改善了灵巧性和抓取能力，同时保持了可扩展性和易用性，并通过端到端数据采集和策略执行管道验证了其能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着对更大规模操作数据集需求的增长，手持式机器人夹爪数据采集和相关夹爪设计变得至关重要。当前的数据采集设备设计倾向于匹配现有机器人夹爪的形态，牺牲了人机工程学和操作性能。因此需要一种能够同时优化数据采集和执行设备的协同设计方法。

Method: 提出了协同设计框架，将平台约束（数据采集和执行）同时纳入设计过程。设计了Koala Gripper系统，包含：(1)新颖的力优化手指/扳机联动机构，具有方向反射质量特性；(2)独特的单片双拇指设计；(3)以用户为中心的人机工程设计。执行手指可反向驱动，有效质量在几十克量级。

Result: 该夹爪能够在广泛的物体上实现稳定抓取，支持有力的工具使用和精确的分拣任务。通过端到端数据采集和策略执行管道（学习从演示）验证了平台的能力。

Conclusion: Koala Gripper系统通过协同设计框架，在保留并行钳口夹爪的可扩展性和易用性的同时，显著提升了灵巧性和抓取能力，为大规模操作数据采集提供了一条可行的新路径。

Abstract: As the demand for larger manipulation datasets grows, handheld robotic gripper data collection and the associated gripper designs become more vital. Current data collection device designs trend towards matching the morphologies of existing robotic grippers, sacrificing ergonomics and manipulation performance. In this paper, we propose a co-design framework that guides the simultaneous development of both data collection and robotic execution devices by weaving both platform constraints into the design process. Through this workflow, we present the Koala Gripper system, a data capture device and robotic gripper platform that improves dexterity and grasp capability compared to parallel jaw grippers while preserving scalability and ease-of-use. The design introduces a novel force-optimized finger/trigger linkage mechanism with directional reflected mass characteristics, a unique monolithic dual-thumb, and user-centered ergonomic design. The design's actuated robotic fingers are backdrivable, with effective mass on the order of tens of grams. We show that these grippers are capable of secure grasps over a wide range of objects, forceful tool use, and precise singulation. We further validate the platform by deploying it with an end-to-end data collection and policy execution pipeline that highlights its capabilities through learning from demonstration. More information available at http://koalagripper.rai-inst.com

</details>


### [86] [Logic-VLA: A Temporal Logic Conditioned Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20556)
*Celina Shiyu Wang,Yiqi Zhao,Junjie Ye,Yue Wang,Jyotirmoy V. Deshmukh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Logic-VLA，一种受形式化需求感知的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型，它在推理时附加信号时序逻辑（STL）规范作为条件。通过语法图STL编码器预训练捕获时序逻辑语义，并采用两阶段策略适应：在满足性演示上进行STL条件监督微调，再通过流匹配代理的轨迹级偏好优化。在四旋翼导航仿真中，STL满足率提升24.8-40.7个百分点，同时名义NL任务成功率最多仅下降1.8个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型虽能遵循自然语言指令，但自然语言无法精确表达安全关键或时空需求。现有方法在需要精确形式化约束（如时序、空间限制）的场景中表现不足，且通常需要为每种规范单独训练策略，缺乏灵活性。

Method: 1) 语法图(STG)编码器预训练：捕获STL的时序逻辑语义；2) 第一阶段：STL条件监督微调（STL-conditioned SFT）在满足性演示上进行；3) 第二阶段：使用流匹配替代Identity Preference Optimization的轨迹级偏好优化，在匹配的满足/违反rollout对上进行训练。

Result: 在闭环四旋翼导航仿真中（随机化逼真环境），对训练未见的STL公式也进行了泛化测试。相较于STL盲基础策略，Logic-VLA将STL满足率提升24.8至40.7个百分点，而名义NL任务成功率最多仅下降1.8个百分点。

Conclusion: 单一VLA模型能够针对不同的形式化需求自适应调整行为，无需为每种规范单独训练策略，在满足形式化需求的同时保持对名义NL任务的良好表现。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models can follow natural-language (NL) task instructions, but such instructions may not precisely specify safety-critical or spatiotemporal requirements on the resulting behavior. We introduce Logic-VLA, a formal-requirement-aware VLA that conditions on Signal Temporal Logic (STL) specifications supplied at inference time. Logic-VLA uses a syntax-graph-based STL encoder pre-trained to capture temporal logic semantics. Policy adaptation proceeds in two stages: STL-conditioned supervised fine-tuning on satisfying demonstrations is followed by trajectory-level preference optimization over matched satisfying-violating rollout pairs using a flow-matching surrogate for Identity Preference Optimization. This formulation improves formal requirement satisfaction while preserving the nominal NL task. We evaluate Logic-VLA in closed-loop quadcopter navigation simulation across randomized photorealistic environments and test generalization to STL formulas unseen during training. Across the evaluation benchmarks, Logic-VLA improves STL satisfaction rate over an STL-blind base policy by 24.8 to 40.7 percentage points (pp) while reducing nominal NL task success by at most 1.8 pp, showing that a single VLA can adapt its behavior to varying formal requirements without requiring a separate policy for each specification.

</details>


### [87] [Nonlinear Model Predictive Control for Trajectory Tracking of Differentially Flat Fixed-Wing Aerial Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20655)
*Nishanth Bobbili,Pratyaksh Rao,Luca Morando,Luca Masci,Giuseppe Loianno*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一框架，将基于微分平坦的轨迹生成与非线性模型预测控制（NMPC）相结合，用于固定翼无人机的规划与控制。此外，在NMPC中引入风感知采样策略，以提高对风扰动的鲁棒性。通过仿真和真实飞行实验验证，该方法在复杂轨迹跟踪精度和鲁棒性上均有提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定翼无人机因非线性动力学、气动极限和环境扰动，其规划与控制具有挑战性。微分平坦虽能高效生成可行轨迹，但以往多与无模型控制器结合，缺乏预测能力且需要大量调参。

Method: 提出一个集成框架：(1) 基于微分平坦的轨迹生成，保证计算效率；(2) 非线性模型预测控制（NMPC）提供预测性与约束感知控制；(3) 风感知采样策略嵌入NMPC，生成考虑风扰动的动态可行参考轨迹，并严格满足气动和控制输入约束。

Result: 通过大量仿真和真实飞行实验，验证了所提框架在复杂轨迹上的跟踪精度和鲁棒性均有改善，尤其在强风条件下，风感知采样策略优势明显。

Conclusion: 将微分平坦轨迹生成与NMPC统一结合，并融入风感知采样，是提升固定翼无人机在扰动环境下轨迹跟踪性能的有效方案。

Abstract: Planning and control of fixed-wing Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) are challenging due to nonlinear dynamics, aerodynamic limits, and environmental disturbances. Differential flatness offers a principled way to generate fast, feasible trajectories, but its use has largely been confined to model-free controllers, which lack predictive capabilities and demand tuning. In this paper, we propose a unified framework that integrates differential flatness-based trajectory generation with Nonlinear Model Predictive Control (NMPC), combining computationally efficient planning with predictive, constraint-aware control. To further improve robustness, we introduce a wind-aware sampling strategy embedded within the NMPC framework, enabling the generation of dynamically feasible reference trajectories that proactively account for wind disturbances while strictly enforcing aerodynamic and control input constraints. We validate the proposed framework through extensive simulations and real-world flight experiments, demonstrating improved tracking accuracy and robustness for complex trajectories, particularly when using the proposed wind-aware sampling strategy under strong wind conditions.

</details>


### [88] [Rethinking Demonstration Unlearning in Imitation Learning for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20784)
*Jiazhuo Li,Yu Zhang,Yiming Fei,Kangkang Dong,Xiaojun Zhu,Houde Liu,Jinze Tao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对机器人模仿学习中的数据删除（unlearning）问题，提出了一种以重训练（retrain）为校准基准的审计框架，从行为和证据两个维度评估编辑后的策略是否真正去除了被删除演示的影响，并通过一致性检验结合两个维度进行假设验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人模仿学习依赖人类演示数据，用户可能事后要求删除某些演示。重新训练是自然的参照方案但成本昂贵，因此需要更廉价的编辑操作。然而现有的机器遗忘指标（如遗忘损失或单一成员攻击）无法真正判断编辑后的策略在闭环控制中删除了什么，缺乏可靠的评估方法。

Method: 提出一种重训练校准的审计框架：（1）行为轴：度量编辑后策略与重训练策略在匹配状态下的动作发散度，并以独立重训练之间的差异作为校准基线；（2）证据轴：对每条演示施加成员攻击，以重训练为基准，同时报告排名和绝对成员损失水平；（3）通过一致性检验将两个轴结合为联合假设，利用一批独立重训练作为参照以在常规显著性水平下进行拒绝检验。

Result: 在三个真实机器人策略类别和两个模拟套件的五项预注册条件下，两个轴在一个检查点上双向分离：编辑可能修复任务行为但证据不变，或减少证据但行为偏离重训练。在ACT支线上，重定向编辑将盲评机器人成功率恢复到18/20次试验。

Conclusion: 两个审计维度（行为与证据）在实验中表现出双向可分离性，说明仅凭单一维度无法完整评估演示遗忘效果，所提的联合审计框架能更全面地刻画编辑后策略与重训练策略的一致性。

Abstract: Imitation learning for robotics depends on human demonstrations, some of which people may later ask to remove. Retraining without them is the natural reference, but its cost grows with policy and dataset scale, motivating cheaper operators that edit a trained policy. Metrics inherited from machine unlearning, such as forgetting loss or a single membership attack, do not establish what an edit removed from a policy acting in closed loop. We therefore introduce a retrain-calibrated audit that reads demonstration unlearning along two axes: behavior, whether the edited policy acts like one retrained without the removed demonstrations, and evidence, whether an auditor can still detect it was trained on them. The behavior axis measures action divergence to that retrain at matched states, calibrated by a floor built from independent retrains, so a policy at the floor is as close to a retrain as retrains are to each other. The evidence axis applies a per-demonstration membership attack against a retrain null, reporting both its rank and its absolute member-loss level, since rank alone accepts operators that inflate member losses past the null. A conformal test then combines both axes into one hypothesis of joint retrain consistency, against a fleet of independent retrains large enough to reject at conventional significance. Across five preregistered conditions on three real-robot policy classes and two simulation suites, the axes dissociate in both directions on one checkpoint, as an edit may repair task behavior while leaving evidence unchanged, or reduce evidence while moving behavior away from retraining. On the ACT arm, a redirect edit restores blind-scored robot success to 18 of 20 trials.

</details>


### [89] [Natural Sit-to-Stand Motion Synthesis For Humanoids via Guided Assistance Curricula and Staged Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20823)
*Meet Pal Singh,Vyankatesh Ashtekar,Ashish Dutta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需演示或参考轨迹、仅通过强化学习从零合成自然类人坐站（STS）运动的方法。利用单策略PPO，结合耦合力/椅子高度课程、逆运动学生成的多样初始与目标姿态采样，以及基于生物力学和最优控制启发的奖励设计，实现了在八种椅子高度下超过97%的平衡站立成功率，并能在较深坐姿下站起。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人从坐姿站起（STS）是一个极具挑战的控制问题，因为存在无穷多种保持平衡的站起方式。已有方法通常依赖演示或参考轨迹，缺乏从零学习自然类人运动的能力，且难以在多种椅子高度下泛化。因此需要一种无需先验轨迹、可自主学习且具有跨高度泛化能力的STS合成方法。

Method: 方法由三个互补组件构成：(i) 耦合力/椅子高度课程——垂直骨盆辅助力辅助早期轨迹探索并随训练衰减，同时逐步解锁更高的椅子，避免过早分布偏移导致泛化崩溃；(ii) 通过逆运动学生成大量初始及目标姿态（跨越八种以上椅子高度）随机采样以增强运动鲁棒性；(iii) 基于生物力学和最优控制研究定义的奖励集，塑造坐起离座时的角动量，并通过质心压力吸引函数实现支撑区域过渡，确保平滑低功耗驱动。

Result: 在确定性无外力评估器上，该策略在八种椅子高度下达到超过97%的平衡站立成功率。策略能够跨椅子高度泛化出平滑运动，并能从比现有技术更深的坐姿姿态成功站起。

Conclusion: 本文证明了通过精心设计的课程学习、多样姿态采样及生物力学启发的奖励塑造，强化学习无需任何演示即可从零合成自然、鲁棒且跨高度泛化的类人STS运动，显著优于现有技术水平。

Abstract: A humanoid has infinitely many ways to stand up from sitting while maintaining balance, making sit-to-stand (STS) a challenging control problem. We synthesise natural humanoid STS motion from scratch using reinforcement learning, without demonstrations or reference trajectories. A single Proximal Policy Optimisation policy learns smooth, human-like rising driven by three complementary components. (i) A coupled force/chair-height curriculum is used. A vertical pelvis-assist force aids early trajectory exploration and decays over training. Taller chairs are unlocked with decaying assisting force. This ensures that the policy masters a viable STS trajectory at each chair height before being exposed to harder ones, avoiding the premature distribution shift that otherwise collapses generalisation. (ii) Motion robustness is achieved by randomly sampling from a large number of inverse kinematics-generated initial and target poses spanning over eight chair heights. (iii) A set of rewards is defined inspired from biomechanics and optimal control studies. They shape the robot's angular momentum for seat-off, and enable support-region transition via centre of pressure attraction function to ensure smooth low-effort actuation. On a deterministic force-free evaluator, the policy attains more than 97% balanced-standing success across eight chair heights. The policy generalises smooth motion across chair heights and enables the robot to rise from substantially deep-seated postures as compared to the state of the art.

</details>


### [90] [Demonstration-Guided Humanoid Stand-Up on an Emulated Deformable Surface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20852)
*Aniruddh Kushwah,Vyankatesh Ashtekar,Ashish Dutta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种参考引导的强化学习框架，使29自由度Unitree G1人形机器人在可变形软地面上生成站立动作，利用硬地面上录制的人类演示。通过修改MuJoCo的solref和solimp参数模拟地形柔顺性，结合参考运动跟踪奖励和显式恢复奖励（骨盆高度、躯干挺直、最终姿态），先在硬地面训练策略，再降低地形刚度继续训练以适应接触穿透导致的支撑力延迟。实验表明该方法能成功完成摔倒到站立的任务，最大接触穿透约40mm，且消融研究表明仅靠参考跟踪不足以成功站立，显式恢复奖励至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在软地面（如沙地、泥土、草地）上执行站立动作时，地面会因接触产生显著变形和穿透，导致支撑力生成延迟，这对传统参考运动跟踪方法构成挑战。现有方法多假设刚性地面，缺乏对可变形地形的适应能力。

Method: 1) 使用MuJoCo的solref和solimp参数建模柔性地基接触；2) 设计包含参考运动跟踪（通过残差关节位置控制）和显式恢复目标（骨盆高度、躯干挺直度、最终姿态）的奖励函数；3) 两阶段训练：先在硬地面训练策略，再降低地形刚度并扩大名义穿透区域继续训练，使策略适应软地面下的延迟支撑力。

Result: 学习到的策略在仿真中成功完成摔倒到站立的任务，达到目标骨盆高度和躯干挺直度，最大接触穿透约40mm。该方法在两个站立序列上均成功实现最终恢复目标，在硬地面和软地面上都表现良好。消融实验表明仅有参考跟踪无法实现成功站立，显式恢复奖励是不可或缺的。

Conclusion: 参考引导结合显式恢复奖励的强化学习框架能有效使策略从硬地面迁移到软地面场景，解决了接触穿透导致的支撑力延迟问题，为可变形地形下人形机器人运动生成提供了可行方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a reference-guided reinforcement learning framework to generate stand-up motion for a 29-DOF Unitree G1 humanoid on deformable soft ground, using a human demonstration recorded on hard ground. The terrain compliance is modelled using solref and solimp parameters from MuJoCo's rigid body soft-contact model. The rewards consists of (i) reference motion tracking through residual joint-position control and (ii) explicit recovery objectives such as pelvis height, torso uprightness, and the final posture. First, the policy is trained with the specified rewards considering hard ground. Next, the terrain stiffness is lowered by updating solref and the nominal surface penetration zone is expanded using solimp. Subsequent training enables the policy to adapt to the delayed support force generation due to significant surface penetration during contact-intensive phases while preserving the original demonstration pattern. The learned policy successfully completes the fallen-to-standing task in simulation, reaching the targeted pelvis height and uprightness, with a maximum contact penetration of approximately 40 mm during the process. The proposed method is demonstrated on two stand-up sequences and successfully achieves the final recovery objective on both hard and soft ground. Ablation studies show that reference tracking alone is insufficient for successful stand-up, and that explicit recovery rewards are essential.

</details>


### [91] [IMU-Free Body-Frame State Estimation with Sparse Scene Flow for Quadcopters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20891)
*Daniel Grønhaug,Sofie Markeset,Mathias Kolberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种纯视觉状态估计系统，适用于X构型四旋翼无人机，配备标准立体相机对但不依赖任何惯性传感器。系统完全在机体坐标系下运行，仅需同步立体图像和电机推力指令。采用连续-离散扩展卡尔曼滤波器在复合流形状态上估计机体位姿、速度、角速度、重力和干扰，以静止场景点为隐式惯性参考。系统还通过四视图（两个立体像对、两个时刻）全光束法平差生成携带位置、速度和联合协方差的稀疏3D点云，并支持空间自适应点云密度分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统无人机状态估计严重依赖IMU和GPS，但在GPS拒止环境或低成本平台上这些传感器不可用或不可靠。本文旨在探索仅依靠视觉（立体相机）和无惯性传感器的情况下，如何在机体坐标系内实现稳健的状态估计，同时避免对任何世界坐标系基础设施的依赖。

Method: 系统由两部分组成：(1) 在复合流形状态上运行的连续-离散EKF，融合立体图像特征和电机推力命令，通过卡方门控仅接纳静止特征点，利用静止场景点作为隐式惯性参考；(2) 基于双时刻双立体像对的四视图全光束法平差，联合估计点云中每点的位置和速度，以滤波器导出的相对位姿为先验。特征点检测使用FAST和Shi-Tomasi，时间跟踪使用SSD和Lucas-Kanade，跨相机匹配使用NCC。点云密度通过外部焦点进行空间自适应分配。

Result: 系统输出机体坐标系状态估计、校准后的位姿变化和稀疏场景流。点云在关注区域密集覆盖而在其他区域稀疏，能够作为下游机体坐标系世界模型的测量源。

Conclusion: 该架构证明纯视觉、无IMU、无GPS的状态估计在四旋翼上是可行的，通过将静止场景点作为隐式惯性参考巧妙地替代了惯性传感器的功能。系统设计为可扩展架构，未来可无缝集成GPS和IMU。

Abstract: We present a vision-only state estimation system for X-configuration quadcopters equipped with a canonical stereo camera pair and no inertial sensors. The system operates entirely in the body frame, requiring only synchronised stereo images and motor thrust commands. A continuous-discrete extended Kalman filter on a composite manifold state $\langle SE(3), \mathbb{R}^3, \ldots \rangle$ maintains estimates of body-frame pose, velocity, angular velocity, gravity, and disturbances, using stationary scene points as implicit inertial references. Feature points are detected (FAST, Shi-Tomasi), tracked temporally (SSD, Lucas-Kanade) and matched across cameras (NCC), with search regions predicted from filter-derived pose and point uncertainty. Chi-squared gating on the normalised innovation admits only stationary points to the filter. The system also produces a sparse 3D point cloud carrying per-point position, velocity and joint covariance. These come from a 4-view (two stereo pairs at two timestamps) full bundle adjustment that jointly estimates position and velocity from stereo disparity and temporal parallax, with the filter-derived relative pose as a prior. Feature points in the EKF do not enter the solver; their information is reflected through the pose prior. Point cloud density is spatially adaptive: an external focus point directs allocation, producing dense coverage in the region of attention and sparse coverage elsewhere. The output is a body-frame state estimate, a calibrated pose change, and a sparse scene flow. It is intended as a measurement source for a downstream world model anchored in the current body frame, without dependence on GPS, IMU, or any world-frame infrastructure, though the architecture accommodates their future integration.

</details>


### [92] [Scalable Distributed Simulation-Based Testing for Automated Driving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20904)
*Christian Geller,Benedikt Haas,Lutz Eckstein*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个端到端、DevOps驱动的框架，用于在轻量级Kubernetes（K3s）集群上自动化CARLA场景测试的构建、部署和分布式执行。通过Helm charts和动态Helmfile清单声明式地组成仿真环境，利用Argo Workflows实现分布式测试工作流。在200个场景的多节点集群评估中，最优配置比顺序基线提速8倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模虚拟场景测试是验证自动驾驶系统（ADS）和智能交通系统（ITS）的关键，但执行包含数千场景的大规模测试套件既耗费人力又难以扩展，需要自动化、可扩展的测试框架来解决这一瓶颈。

Method: 1) 将ROS 2应用打包为从仓库规范生成的标准Kubernetes Helm charts；2) 通过动态Helmfile清单声明式地组合整个仿真环境；3) 在Argo Workflows中实现分布式测试工作流，包括环境供给、OpenSCENARIO测试用例聚合与批处理、跨集群节点并行执行场景、日志与资源指标收集。

Result: 在多节点K3s集群上运行200个场景的评估中，最优配置将端到端工作流时间比顺序基线加速8倍以上，并量化了并行度、编排开销与集群稳定性之间的权衡。此外，在真实ADS测试应用中进行了演示，连接了场景源和下游评估模块。

Conclusion: 该框架不仅为可扩展的仿真测试提供了坚实基础，还能生成可追踪的证据以支持安全论证，展示了在真实ADS测试中的实用性。

Abstract: Virtual scenario-based testing is a key enabler for validating automated driving systems (ADS) and intelligent transport systems (ITS). However, executing large-scale test suites involving possibly thousands of scenarios remains labor-intensive and difficult to scale. This paper presents an end-to-end, DevOps-driven framework that automates build, deployment, and distributed execution of CARLA-based scenario tests of an ADS on a lightweight Kubernetes cluster. ROS 2 applications are packaged as standardized Kubernetes Helm charts generated from repository specifications, while entire simulation environments are composed declaratively via dynamic Helmfile manifests. The paper describes how a distributed testing workflow can be implemented in Argo Workflows to provision environments, aggregate and batch OpenSCENARIO test cases from configurable sources, execute scenarios in parallel across cluster nodes, and collect logs and resource metrics. In an evaluation on a multi-node K3s cluster running 200 scenarios, the best configuration speeds up end-to-end workflow time by more than a factor of eight compared to a sequential baseline. The results demonstrate significant gains in end-to-end execution time and quantify trade-offs between parallelism, orchestration overhead, and cluster stability. The framework is further demonstrated in a real-world ADS test application with connections to scenario sources and downstream evaluation modules. This demonstrates that the approach provides a strong foundation not only for scalable simulation testing, but also for generating traceable evidence that can support safety arguments.

</details>


### [93] [Fast Coordinated Bimanual Motion Planning With Hard Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20946)
*Borna Paro,Luka Petrović,Ivan Marković*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种快速双臂操作运动规划管线，采用领导者-跟随者参数化方法，将跟随者末端通过逆运动学求解以满足刚性物体操作中两末端执行器间的固定相对变换约束。该方法在仿真中比先前工作快19.4倍，并在真实Kinova Gen3双臂平台上成功验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双臂操作虽能完成复杂任务，但高自由度带来复杂性。刚性物体操作要求两末端执行器之间相对变换在整个运动过程中保持固定，这表现为非线性等式约束，将可行配置空间限制在零测度流形上，给传统运动规划器带来巨大挑战。

Method: 提出快速双臂运动规划管线，通过领导者-跟随者参数化强制执行硬变换约束：将领导者的配置作为自由变量，跟随者的配置则通过逆运动学求解以满足约束，从而在整个路径上连续满足约束条件。

Result: 在多样化环境、约束和双臂平台的仿真中广泛评估，相比先前工作规划速度提升19.4倍，同时保证连续约束满足。在真实双臂Kinova Gen3平台上进行托盘搬运和长物体操作的实验，验证了规划轨迹可直接迁移到物理硬件。

Conclusion: 该领导者-跟随者参数化方法能够高效处理双臂刚性物体的硬约束规划问题，显著提升规划速度并保证约束的连续满足，且规划结果可直接迁移至真实硬件执行。

Abstract: Bimanual manipulation enables complex tasks but introduces added complexity from the high number of degrees of freedom involved. When handling rigid objects, the relative transformation between the two end effectors must remain fixed throughout the motion, manifesting as a nonlinear equality constraint that confines the feasible configuration space to a measure-zero manifold and challenges conventional motion planners. We propose a fast bimanual motion planning pipeline that enforces this hard transformation constraint continuously along the entire path, using a leader-follower parameterization: the leader's configuration is treated as a free variable, while the follower's is determined via inverse kinematics to satisfy the constraint. We extensively evaluate the method in simulation across diverse environments, constraints and bimanual platforms, achieving 19.4x faster planning than prior work while guaranteeing continuous constraint satisfaction. Real-world experiments on a bimanual Kinova Gen3 setup, involving tray transport and elongated-object manipulation, validate direct transfer of planned trajectories to physical hardware.

</details>


### [94] [Neural-Primitive: An Efficient End-to-end Local Planner with Primitive-based Imitation Learning for Autonomous Flight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20948)
*Zhitao Liu,Guangtong Xu,Zihan Wang,Jialiang Hou,Chao Xu,Fei Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于模仿学习的端到端局部规划器，通过轻量级离线基元数据集收集框架生成安全高质量的轨迹基元，利用紧凑神经网络直接将传感器输入映射为多项式系数，无需后端求解即可实时生成平滑、无碰撞且动力学可行的轨迹，计算速度低于1ms，内存占用小于1.5MiB，并在真实环境中验证了零样本迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主飞行在未知杂乱环境中面临机载轨迹生成的计算-质量-内存三难问题，现有方法难以同时兼顾实时性、轨迹质量与低内存开销。

Method: 提出端到端局部规划器：1) 设计轻量级基于离线基元的数据集收集框架，在非凸环境中生成安全高质量的轨迹基元；2) 使用紧凑神经网络直接将传感器输入映射为多项式系数，这些系数天然编码高阶动力学信息，避免后端优化求解。

Result: 在标准桌面端计算时间低于1ms，机载飞行平均3.68ms；内存占用小于1.5MiB；仿真基准测试在规划延迟和目标到达进度质量上均展现出优越性；真实世界实验验证了零样本sim-to-real迁移能力。

Conclusion: 该方法成功解决了机载轨迹生成的计算-质量-内存三难问题，实现了超快计算、低内存占用和高质量轨迹的兼顾，并具有良好的真实世界部署鲁棒性。

Abstract: Autonomous flight in unknown cluttered environments is hindered by the computation-quality-memory trilemma of onboard trajectory generation. In this paper, we propose an efficient end-to-end local planner via imitation learning. A lightweight offline-primitive-based dataset collection framework is designed to produce safe and high-quality trajectory primitives in non-convex environments. A compact neural network directly maps sensory inputs to polynomial coefficients that inherently encode higher-order dynamical information. The learned policy generates smooth, empirically collision-free and dynamically feasible trajectories in real time without back-end solving. It achieves ultra-fast computation (below 1ms on a standard desktop and average 3.68ms during onboard flight), while maintaining low onboard memory requirements (less than 1.5MiB). Extensive simulation benchmarks demonstrate superiority in both planning latency and target-reaching progress quality. Zero-shot deployment in real-world experiments further validates the robust sim-to-real transfer capability of the proposed method.

</details>


### [95] [Hybrid Roller-Jamming Gripper for Object Acquisition and Retention Under Pose Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20962)
*Yijie Ren,Guillaume Gourmelen,Hiroyasu Iwata*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合滚筒-颗粒阻塞（hybrid roller-jamming）夹爪，通过滚筒向内旋转主动将物体拉入夹爪中心，再利用真空诱导的颗粒阻塞使滚筒硬化并稳定抓取，解决了家庭场景中姿态不确定导致的偏心或部分接触问题。在840次抓取试验中成功812次（96.7%），消融实验表明滚筒和阻塞机制各自贡献不同功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家庭操作中物体姿态不确定常导致初始接触偏心或不完整，使可靠的物体抓取变得困难。现有方案中，滚筒式夹爪能主动将物体吸入但抓取后稳定性不足；颗粒阻塞式夹爪则需要充分接触才能实现强保持。因此需要一种能同时解决主动吸入和抓取后保持的方案。

Method: 设计混合滚筒-阻塞夹爪：首先通过向内旋转的滚筒增加与物体的接触面积并将物体拉向夹爪中心，然后通过真空诱导颗粒阻塞使滚筒硬化，从而稳定抓取。论文还提出了简化的几何分析以及力能力的台式特性化，并将原型安装在7自由度机械臂上进行评估。

Result: 使用8个测试物体，进行了840次抓取试验（216次平面偏移和624次姿态偏移），成功812/840次（215/216平面偏移，597/624姿态偏移）。消融实验162次试验中，仅滚筒和仅阻塞条件分别成功54/81和24/81，表明两者贡献不同。

Conclusion: 结果提供了机制层面的初步证据，表明混合滚筒-阻塞策略是改善不完全首次接触后抓取获取与保持的可行方案，是一种有前景的抓取策略。

Abstract: In household manipulation, pose uncertainty often results in off-centre or partial initial contact, making reliable object acquisition difficult. Roller-based grippers can actively draw objects inward but often provide limited post-capture stability, whereas granular-jamming grippers require sufficient contact before jamming to achieve strong retention. This paper presents a hybrid roller-jamming gripper that integrates active object intake and post-capture retention within a single gripper. The proposed gripper uses inward roller rotation to increase contact and draw the object toward the gripper centre, followed by vacuum-induced granular jamming to stiffen the rollers and stabilise the grasp. The paper also presents a simplified geometric analysis of the gripper and a bench-level characterisation of the prototype's force capability. The gripper prototype was mounted on a 7-DoF robotic arm and evaluated using eight test objects. Furthermore, controlled planar position and orientation offsets were applied, with each condition repeated three times. The main evaluation comprised 840 grasp trials, including 216 planar-offset trials and 624 orientation-offset trials. Overall, the gripper succeeded in 812/840 trials: 215/216 planar-offset trials and 597/624 orientation-offset trials. The ablation evaluation comprised 162 trials on three objects. The roller-only and jamming-only conditions achieved 54/81 and 24/81 successes, respectively, showing their different contributions. These results provide initial mechanism-level evidence that hybrid roller-jamming is a promising strategy for improving acquisition and retention after imperfect first contact.

</details>


### [96] [PhysCaP: Grounding Code-as-Policy Agent with Physics-Informed Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21031)
*Chen-Yu Lin,Jing-Wen Chen,Hsueh-En Chang,Hung-An Chen,Sheng-Hsun Chang,Chi-Pin Huang,Fu-En Yang,Min-Hung Chen,Yi-Ting Chen,Yu-Chiang Frank Wang,Shao-Hua Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PhysCaP是一个物理信息驱动的Code-as-Policy智能体，用于机器人操作中的主动感知。它通过物理信息探索层显式获取信息，利用训练无关的物理属性提取模块（估计物体质量与刚度），并采用双智能体设计（Planner决定何时探索，Prioritizer筛选和排序交互）来平衡探索成本与信息效率。在真实桌面操作任务和LIBERO模拟任务中，PhysCaP以更少的交互和更短的时间达到了与基线相当或更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作策略虽然在模仿演示方面表现出色，但依赖被动观察，无法推断对操作至关重要的潜在物理属性（如质量、刚度等）。这限制了其在隐藏物理属性场景下的表现。

Method: PhysCaP包含三个核心组件：(1) 物理信息探索层，在code-as-policy框架中实现显式信息寻求；(2) 训练无关的物理属性提取模块，仅从机器人本体感觉估计物体质量和刚度，无需额外传感器；(3) 双智能体设计——Planner决定何时探索/停止，Prioritizer过滤不合理的交互并用启发式优先级分数排序剩余交互，实现高效、有针对性的探索。

Result: 在真实桌面操作任务（寻找隐藏物体、检测空罐、寻找成熟牛油果）和LIBERO模拟任务中，PhysCaP在较少的交互次数和更短的执行时间内达到或超过了现有被动和朴素交互基线的性能。消融实验验证了所提物理属性提取模块的有效性。

Conclusion: 主动感知（通过交互获取物理信息）比被动观察更有效地解决隐藏物理属性问题，而精心设计的探索策略（而非盲目交互）是提升效率的关键。

Abstract: We present PhysCaP, a Physics-Informed Code-as-Policy agent for active perception in robotic manipulation. While vision-language-action policies excel at imitating demonstrations, they rely on passive observation and fail to infer latent physical properties critical for manipulation. PhysCaP augments code-as-policy frameworks with a physics-informed exploration layer that enables explicit information-seeking through interaction. It introduces training-free physical property extraction modules that estimate object mass and stiffness from robot proprioception without additional sensors. To balance exploration costs and the efficiency of information obtained, PhysCaP employs a dual-agent design: a Planner that decides when to explore and when to stop, and a Prioritizer that filters implausible interactions and ranks the remainder using a heuristic priority score, enabling efficient, targeted exploration. We evaluate PhysCaP on real-world tabletop manipulation tasks (searching for hidden objects, detecting empty cans, and finding ripe avocados) and a simulated task in LIBERO. The results show that existing passive and naive interactive baselines either fail when physical properties are hidden or over-explore, whereas PhysCaP achieves comparable performance with fewer interactions and reduced execution time. Ablation studies further validate the effectiveness of the proposed physical property extraction modules. Project page: https://physcap.github.io

</details>


### [97] [Roadside-Cooperative Autonomous Driving: From Data Platform to Vision-Language End-to-End Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21032)
*Yitao Xu,Tong Wu,Yiyan Wu,Guoji Xu,Yanbo Jiang,Jiahao Wang,Zehong Ke,Junkai Jiang,Fang Zhang,Jianqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出V2XBench仿真平台和Chat-V2XBench VQA数据集，并在此基础上提出端到端协同驾驶框架AURORA，通过双视角感知架构和跨视角查询对齐融合模块，结合LoRA微调的视觉语言模型实现推理与轨迹规划，在严重遮挡场景下达到SOTA性能（路线完成率98.21%，驾驶评分76.02），且通信带宽需求低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有V2X基准缺乏闭环评估和语言监督支持，阻碍了视觉语言模型在端到端协同驾驶中的应用与发展。

Method: 1) 构建V2XBench仿真平台，支持同步车路感知与闭环评估；2) 构建Chat-V2XBench渐进式VQA数据集；3) 提出AURORA框架，包含双视角感知架构、CQAF跨视角查询对齐融合模块，以及LoRA适配的VLM实现语义推理到生成式轨迹规划的桥接。

Result: 在V2XBench闭环评估中，AURORA在严重遮挡场景下达到SOTA性能：路线完成率98.21%，驾驶评分76.02，同时保持低路侧通信带宽需求。

Conclusion: 本工作开创了可扩展的V2X-VLM范式，为下一代协同自动驾驶铺平道路。

Abstract: Vehicle-to-Everything (V2X) cooperation enables beyond-line-of-sight perception, mitigating occlusions in single-vehicle sensing. However, existing V2X benchmarks provide limited support for closed-loop evaluation and language-grounded supervision, hindering the development of vision-language models (VLMs) for end-to-end cooperative driving. To address these limitations, we introduce V2XBench, a simulation platform featuring synchronized ego--roadside sensing and closed-loop evaluation, together with Chat-V2XBench, a progressively structured VQA dataset for cooperative reasoning. Building upon this benchmark infrastructure, we propose AURORA, an end-to-end cooperative driving framework. Equipped with a dual-view perception architecture, AURORA mitigates spatial and semantic discrepancies across ego and roadside viewpoints through a query-level Cross-View Query Alignment and Fusion (CQAF) module. Leveraging the resulting unified tokens, a LoRA-adapted VLM bridges semantic reasoning and generative trajectory planning. Extensive closed-loop evaluations on V2XBench demonstrate that AURORA achieves state-of-the-art performance in heavily occluded scenarios, with a Route Completion rate of 98.21% and a Driving Score of 76.02, while requiring low roadside communication bandwidth. Ultimately, this work pioneers an extensible V2X--VLM paradigm, paving the way for next-generation cooperative autonomous driving.

</details>


### [98] [TaPeR: Probabilistic Recovery of Sparse Task Precedence Graphs from a Handful of Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21035)
*Adrian Röfer,Karla Stepanova,Abhinav Valada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅从运动学图和物体相对位姿分布中提取任务依赖结构的方法，无需符号谓词监督，能从较少的演示中恢复更准确的任务依赖图，并生成多种有效的机器人执行顺序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程操作任务往往只有部分有序依赖关系，恢复这些依赖能使机器人灵活重排子任务。现有方法通常需要时序和符号双重监督，但符号谓词在实际场景中难以显式接地（grounding），这限制了其应用。

Method: 该方法仅使用简单的运动学图和物体相对位姿分布来表示演示。首先估计每对任务步骤之间的依赖概率，用这些概率初始化一个优先级图的边权重，然后通过一个过滤管道将概率图转换为最终的任务依赖图。

Result: 在现有基准和包含更长任务、更复杂依赖的新数据集上评估，该方法比基线方法用更少的演示恢复出更准确的任务结构，并能生成同一任务的多种有效机器人执行顺序。

Conclusion: 仅依靠运动学图和物体相对位姿分布即可有效提取任务依赖结构，避免了符号谓词接地难题，为机器人灵活重排子任务提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Long-horizon manipulation tasks are often only partially ordered. For example, when assembling an electronic device, the battery and circuit board may be installed in either order, but both must be in place before the enclosure is closed. Recovering such dependencies enables robots to flexibly reorder subtasks while preserving task validity. Existing approaches typically infer task structure from human demonstrations using both temporal and symbolic supervision. However, symbolic predicates require explicit grounding, which is difficult to obtain in realistic settings. In this work, we present an approach for extracting task dependency structures from demonstrations using only simple kinematic graphs and distributions over relative object poses. From these representations, our method estimates pairwise task-step-dependency probabilities and uses them to initialize the edge weights of a precedence graph. We then introduce a filtering pipeline that converts this graph of probability estimates into the final task dependency graph. We evaluate our approach on an existing benchmark and on a new dataset comprising longer tasks with more complex dependencies. We find that our method recovers more accurate task structures from fewer demonstrations than the baselines. Finally, we demonstrate that the inferred graphs can be used to generate multiple valid robotic execution orders for the same task.

</details>


### [99] [FF-MPCC: High-speed Agile Formation Flight with Model Predictive Contouring Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21056)
*Aditya Dandwate,Vit Kratky,Parakh M. Gupta,Martin Saska,Robert Penicka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新颖的分布式无人机编队飞行方法，将编队保持集成到MPCC（模型预测轮廓控制）框架中，使无人机在沿复杂路径飞行、遵守各自动力学约束的同时保持期望编队。通过引入动态编队几何的重新参数化和同步方法，实现了高速路径跟踪下编队完整性的保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机在给定路径上以敏捷方式保持预设编队飞行仍具挑战性，尤其是在需要以平台极限飞行的极具挑战性轨迹上。现有时间参数化轨迹跟踪方法难以在高速、复杂路径下同时满足个体动力学约束和编队完整性要求。

Method: 提出一种分布式的编队飞行方法，核心贡献包括：(1) 将编队保持集成到MPCC框架中；(2) 引入新颖的动态编队几何重新参数化与同步方法；(3) 提出分布式方法确定各无人机期望位置。该方法允许无人机在沿复杂路径行进时自适应调整进度，同时满足个体动力学约束并保持期望编队。

Result: 通过广泛的仿真和实际实验验证，覆盖不同复杂度路径和飞行中编队形状变换多种场景。与时间参数化轨迹跟踪相比，在高达21 m/s的高速飞行中编队保持性能提升65%，同时到达目标所需时间相当。

Conclusion: 所提出的分布式MPCC编队控制方法能够在高速复杂路径跟踪下有效协调编队飞行，显著提升编队保持精度而不牺牲任务效率，证明了该方法的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: Flying in a prescribed formation in an agile manner remains a challenging problem in the field of UAVs, particularly when following highly-demanding trajectories that require flight at platform limits. We address this problem by proposing a novel decentralized approach to formation flight along a given path that integrates formation maintenance into the MPCC framework, allowing UAVs to adapt their progression along complex paths while respecting individual dynamic constraints and maintaining the desired formation. To this end, we introduce a novel reparametrization and synchronization method for dynamic formation geometries together with a decentralized approach to determine the desired positions for the individual UAVs. The proposed approach allows the formation to coordinate high-speed path following without compromising formation integrity. The proposed approach is validated through extensive simulation and real-world experiments involving scenarios with varying complexity of paths and changes of required formation shape on the fly. In comparison to time-parameterized trajectory tracking, we demonstrate improved formation maintenance by 65% in high-speed flight with velocities up to 21 m/s, while achieving comparable times required to reach the goal.

</details>


### [100] [Teaching is a Process: The TOSS Framework for Modeling Human Teaching Decisions in Human-Interactive Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21083)
*Bernhard Hilpert,Kim Baraka,Joost Broekens*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过一项探索性、自下而上的研究（N=34），分析了204条人类在机器人强化学习场景中的直觉教学回应，揭示了教学决策由触发因素、目标、信号和策略构成的互联网络，并提出TOSS框架将人机教学概念化为一个过程循环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成功的机器人教学需要机器人的处理需求与人类教学意图之间保持对齐。为了更好地理解这种对齐，需要揭示人类在教学时直觉应用的底层逻辑。

Method: 采用探索性、自下而上的研究方法，让34名参与者观察两个不同的机器人强化学习场景，收集并分析204条涵盖早期、中期和晚期学习阶段的教学回应，从中归纳教学决策的结构。

Result: 教学决策由一个相互关联的复杂网络构成，包含触发因素（情境催化剂）、目标（主观教学目标）、信号（沟通行为）和策略（高层治理）。教师会自发扮演教练、工程师或设计师等不同角色，并优先考虑不同的目标。

Conclusion: 提出TOSS框架，将人机教学概念化为机器人行为与人类教学行为之间的程序循环，其中人类教学决策被建模为由教学目标与策略调制的触发-信号响应。该框架提供公开数据集和理论基础，用于理解教学决策、模拟现实oracle，以及设计以人为中心的教学设置和超越现有约束的新型机器人学习算法。

Abstract: Successful Human-Robot Teaching assumes alignment between robot processing needs and human teaching intent. To better understand this alignment, this work seeks to uncover the underlying logic that humans intuitively apply when teaching. Through an exploratory, bottom-up study with N=34, participants observing two distinct robot Reinforcement Learning (RL) scenarios, we analyze 204 intuitive teaching responses across early, middle, and late learning phases. Results reveal that teaching decisions consist of a nuanced, interconnected network of Triggers (situational catalysts), Objectives (subjective teaching targets), Signals (communicative acts), and Strategies (high-level governance) in which teachers spontaneously adopt diverse roles, acting as coaches, engineers, or designers and prioritize different objectives. Based on these results, we introduce the TOSS Framework, which conceptualizes Human-Robot teaching as a procedural loop between robot behavior and human teaching actions, in which human teaching decisions are modeled as Trigger-Signal responses modulated by teaching Objectives and Strategies. It provides future research with an openly accessible dataset and a theoretical foundation for a) understanding teaching decisions and b) simulating realistic oracles as well as c) designing human-centered teaching settings and novel robot learning algorithms that go beyond the constraints of current robot learning settings.

</details>


### [101] [SRL-MPC: Shape-Aware Reinforcement Learned Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21175)
*Ruihua Han,Rui Gao,Zhe Liu,Xinyi Wang,Chang Chen,Shuai Wang,Qi Hao,Jia Pan,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SRL-MPC方法，结合高阶级控制屏障函数(HOCBF)与强化学习(RL)，实现异构形状机器人在密集人群中的安全、高效、自适应导航，无需几何简化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统导航方法假设机器人同质、工作空间稀疏、几何简化、离线计算或手工参数，难以应对密集且异构的人群场景，缺乏形状感知的安全性。

Method: 利用支撑函数变换下的几何分离特征(GSFs)构建HOCBF安全约束；RL框架学习神经策略，读取GSFs并实时输出MPC参数更新，使求解器自适应周围人群几何形状。

Result: 在随机人群场景及任意形状机器人编队实验中，SRL-MPC在安全性与适应性上显著优于代表性基线方法，展现出有效性、可扩展性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SRL-MPC保留了MPC的安全结构与泛化性，同时融合RL的适应性与智能性，为异构形状人群导航提供了更可靠的方案。

Abstract: Safe and efficient shape-aware navigation in heterogeneous crowds and robot fleets remains challenging. Traditional approaches often assume homogeneous robots, sparse workspaces, simplified geometry, offline computation, or handcrafted parameters to make the problem tractable, which limits their deployment in dense crowd scenarios. Toward this end, we propose Shape-Aware Reinforcement Learned Model Predictive Control (SRL-MPC), a method for safe, efficient, and adaptive navigation in crowds with heterogeneous shapes without geometry simplification. To encode shape-aware safety, we formulate high-order control barrier function (HOCBF) constraints from geometric separation features (GSFs) based on support function transformation. A reinforcement learning (RL) framework then learns a neural policy that reads GSFs and outputs real-time MPC parameter updates, enabling the MPC solver to adapt to neighboring crowd geometries. The key advantage of SRL-MPC is that it preserves the safety structure and generalizability of MPC while integrating the adaptability and intelligence of RL. Experiments in randomized crowd scenarios with arbitrary shaped robot fleets demonstrate the effectiveness, scalability, and robustness of SRL-MPC.
