The results show that SRL-MPC substantially outperforms representative baselines in safety and adaptability. Project website: https://hanruihua.github.io/srl_mpc_project/

</details>


### [102] [Beyond Imitation: Self-Improving Robot Policies via Off-Policy Q-Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21204)
*Varun Giridhar,Anant Khandelwal,Jeremy A. Collins,Ignat Georgiev,Animesh Garg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Q-Planning方法，为大规模视觉运动BC策略配备一个小型离策略Q函数，利用Q函数评估价值而非模仿动作的对称性差异，实现推理时的价值引导动作选择以及仅微调Q函数的在线自改进，在多个基准和真机任务上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为克隆（BC）虽然推动了机器人操作领域的显著进展，但其基本局限在于无法自我改进：策略失败后无法从失败中学习而不需要额外的人类示范。强化学习微调虽提供自我改进路径，但难以扩展到现代机器人策略所依赖的数十亿参数模型。

Method: Q-Planning将大型视觉运动BC策略与小型离策略Q函数相结合。由于Q函数估计价值而非模仿动作，它可以用与BC策略相同的成功示范进行训练，之后还能吸收成功和失败的部署回放数据。方法利用这一非对称性实现推理时的价值引导动作选择（对BC采样进行单步Q加权平均），以及在线自我改进（仅微调Q函数，保持BC权重不变）。

Result: 在LIBERO上十次自我改进迭代将所有基准得分提升（LIBERO-10从93%提升至99%），RoboTwin从83.8%提升至91.4%；在接触丰富的双臂真机任务上，stack-cups从40%提升至90%，insert-wallet从25%提升至80%（五次迭代）；而仅对成功回放做SFT的方法停滞在55%和30%。在相同在线预算下，Q-Planning是Best-of-N、过滤SFT、IBRL、DSRL和DAWR中唯一能在不训练辅助actor的情况下稳定从失败中改进的方法。

Conclusion: Q-Planning通过将价值估计与行为克隆解耦，实现了大规模机器人策略的稳定在线自我改进，在不触碰BC权重的条件下，利用失败回放数据持续提升性能，为机器人操作中的自我改进提供了一种可扩展且有效的新范式。

Abstract: Behaviour Cloning (BC) has driven remarkable progress in robot manipulation, yet it is fundamentally limited by its inability to self-improve: a policy that fails cannot learn from that failure without additional human demonstrations. Reinforcement Learning fine-tuning offers a path to self-improvement but has proven difficult to scale to the multi-billion-parameter models underpinning modern robot policies. We propose Q-Planning, which equips a large visuomotor BC policy with a small off-policy Q-function. Because a Q-function estimates value rather than imitates actions, it can be trained on the same successful demonstrations as the BC policy and later absorb both successful and failed deployment rollouts, an asymmetry BC does not have. We exploit this asymmetry to enable value-guided action selection at inference (a single-step Q-weighted average over BC draws) and online self-improvement that fine-tunes only the Q-function, leaving the BC weights untouched. On LIBERO and bimanual RoboTwin, ten iterations of self-improvement lift every benchmark score we tested (LIBERO-10 93% to 99%, RoboTwin 83.8% to 91.4%) and shorten successful episodes on the near-ceiling suites (LIBERO-Object, LIBERO-Goal). On two contact-rich bimanual real-robot tasks, the same loop (BC frozen, no human intervention) improves purely from its own deployment rollouts: stack-cups 40% to 90% and insert-wallet 25% to 80% in five iterations, whereas SFT on successful rollouts alone stalls at 55% and 30%. Under an identical online budget Q-Planning is the only method, among Best-of-N, filtered SFT, IBRL, DSRL, and DAWR, that improves stably from failures without training an auxiliary actor.

</details>


### [103] [The Coastline as a Structural Constraint: Harnessing Scene Geometry for Autonomous Surface Vessel Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21276)
*Derek R. Benham,Joshua G. Mangelson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出两种互补的GPS拒止环境下的沿海水面无人艇定位框架：一种基于LiDAR观测水面估计横滚、俯仰和垂荡，并通过岸线配准恢复全局位置和航向；另一种仅依赖被动图像进行语义分割检测岸线和地平线，通过单目几何推断岸线距离，构建局部子地图并在层级因子图中与卫星海岸线地图配准融合。在三个真实沿海数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 沿海环境蕴含着丰富且尚未充分开发的地理几何结构，可为全球参照定位提供线索。在GPS拒止条件下，需要利用这些天然几何特征实现无人水面艇的可靠定位。

Method: 1) LiDAR框架：利用水面点云估计横滚、俯仰和垂荡，通过岸线观测与卫星海岸线地图的直接配准恢复全局位置和航向。2) 单目视觉框架：通过语义分割检测岸线和地平线，利用沿海场景几何从单目图像推断岸线距离，将岸线观测累积为短时局部子地图，与卫星海岸线地图配准，并在层级因子图中进行融合。

Result: 在三个真实沿海数据集上，LiDAR管道持续优于标准基线的轨迹精度；单目架构保持有界的长时漂移；并证实了现代零样本基础模型能够可靠提取不同沿海环境的岸线观测。

Conclusion: 沿海几何为GPS拒止的海上定位提供了强大且可靠的全球参照信息来源，两种框架协同证明了该方法的实用性与通用性。

Abstract: Coastal environments contain rich, largely unexploited geometric structure capable of providing globally referenced localization cues. In this work, we present two complementary localization frameworks that exploit shoreline and water-surface geometry for GPS-denied autonomous surface vessel localization. The first framework leverages LiDAR observations of the water surface to estimate roll, pitch, and heave (vertical motion), while recovering global position and heading through direct registration of shoreline observations against a satellite-derived coastline map. The second framework relies solely on passive imagery to detect the shoreline and horizon through semantic segmentation. Using the proposed coastal scene geometry, shoreline distance is inferred from monocular imagery. Shoreline observations are accumulated into short-duration local submaps, registered against the same satellite-derived coastline map, and fused within a hierarchical factor graph. Evaluated across three real-world coastal datasets, the LiDAR pipeline consistently improves trajectory accuracy over standard baselines, while the monocular architecture maintains bounded long-term drift. In addition, we establish that modern zero-shot foundation models can reliably extract shoreline observations across diverse coastal environments. Together, these results demonstrate that coastal geometry provides a powerful and dependable source of globally referenced information for GPS-denied maritime localization.

</details>


### [104] [VT-MUSE: Multimodal Unified Sequential Visuotactile Representation Learning for Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21290)
*Congsheng Xu,Qiaochu Yang,Fangyuan Shi,Yifan Han,Baijun Chen,Yiming Wang,Haonan Zhao,Daolin Ma,Xiaokang Yang,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VT-MUSE是一个用于视觉触觉操作的多模态统一序列表示学习框架，通过两阶段学习（跨模态时间对齐与掩码一致性、条件变分潜变量模型）同时捕捉细粒度跨模态依赖和时间演化接触信息，在仿真和真实实验中均显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常独立编码视觉和触觉观测后再进行融合，难以捕捉细粒度的跨模态依赖；且大多只关注当前时间步的观测，忽略了接触的时间演化信息。

Method: 两阶段表示学习框架：阶段一通过跨模态时间对齐和掩码视图一致性联合适配各模态专属编码器；阶段二使用条件变分潜变量模型处理掩码视觉序列与完整触觉历史，辅助解码器重建近期掩码视觉观测并预测触觉深度变化，最终通过门控交叉注意力将所学表示集成到轻量Transformer策略中。

Result: 在仿真基准上，VT-MUSE在所有任务上比最强基线平均高出11个百分点，并在真实世界实验中取得了实质性改进。

Conclusion: VT-MUSE通过统一的序列表示学习有效解决了跨模态依赖和时序接触演化两大挑战，证明了同时建模视觉-触觉细粒度交互与时间动态对操作任务的重要性。

Abstract: We propose VT-MUSE, a Multimodal Unified SEquential representation learning framework for visuotactilemanipulation. Existing approaches often encode visual and tactile observations independently before fusion, limiting their ability to capture fine-grained cross-modal dependencies. Moreover, most methods focus on observations at the current time step and overlook the temporal evolution of contact. VT-MUSE addresses both limitations through a two-stage representation learning framework. In Stage I, modality specific encoders are jointly adapted via cross-modal temporal alignment and masked-view consistency. In Stage II, a conditional variational latent model processes masked visual sequences together with full tactile histories. Auxiliary decoders reconstruct the masked recent visual observations and predict tactile depth changes, encouraging the latent representation to retain both global visual context and local contact dynamics. The learned representation is subsequently integrated into a lightweight Transformer policy through gated cross-attention. On the simulation benchmark, VT-MUSE outperforms the strongest baseline evaluated on all tasks by 11 percentage points and also achieves substantial improvements in real-world experiments.

</details>


### [105] [NeSAM: Neuro-Symbolic Kinodynamics with Soil Adaptation for Off-Road Mobility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21330)
*Chenhui Pan,Tong Xu,Francesco Cancelliere,Xuesu Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出NeSAM，一个结合可微Bekker-Wong土力学模型、学习地形表征和Transformer残差动力学模型的神经符号框架，用于越野车辆六自由度运动学预测，在仿真和实物中预测精度分别提升30%和29%，轨迹跟踪距离降低69.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越野车辆在可变形地形上的运动预测具有挑战性，因为下沉量、滑移和牵引力会随局部土壤条件变化。现有的学习型运动模型虽然直接近似车辆-地形交互，但缺乏对土壤力学的显式建模，物理可解释性有限。

Method: NeSAM采用神经符号框架，包含三个关键组件：(1) 可微Bekker-Wong土力学模型用于建模土壤依赖的交互力；(2) 学习得到的地形表征；(3) Transformer残差动力学模型纠正解析预测与观测车辆动力学之间的差异。此外，NeSAM通过扩展卡尔曼滤波器从地形观测中估计并在线更新物理意义的土壤参数。

Result: 在基于Chrono多物理引擎的Verti-Bench模拟器及物理Verti-4-Wheeler平台上验证，NeSAM在仿真中预测精度提升最高30%，实物数据上提升29%。集成闭环导航控制器后，通过在线土壤自适应将穿越成功率提升，并将参考轨迹的Hausdorff距离降低69.4%。

Conclusion: NeSAM通过将物理可解释的土力学模型与学习型残差模型相结合，在保持物理可解释性的同时显著提升了越野车辆的运动预测精度，并通过在线土壤参数估计增强了长时程预测和闭环导航能力。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of off-road vehicle motion over deformable terrain remains challenging because sinkage, slip, and traction vary with local soil conditions. Existing learning-based kinodynamic models directly approximate vehicle-terrain interactions from data but do not explicitly represent soil mechanics and offer limited physical interpretability. To address these limitations, we present NeSAM, a neuro-symbolic framework that combines differentiable Bekker-Wong terramechanics with learned terrain representations and a Transformer-based residual dynamics model for long-horizon, six degree-of-freedom kinodynamic prediction. The terramechanics component models soil-dependent interaction forces, while the residual model corrects discrepancies between the analytical prediction and the observed vehicle dynamics. NeSAM further estimates physically meaningful soil parameters from terrain observations and updates them online using an extended Kalman filter. We evaluate NeSAM in Verti-Bench, a simulator built on the Chrono multiphysics engine, and validate its performance on a physical Verti-4-Wheeler platform. NeSAM improves prediction accuracy by up to 30% in simulation and 29% on real-world data relative to the strongest compared baselines. When integrated with a close-loop navigation controller, NeSAM further improves traversal success rate through online soil adaptation while reduces Hausdorff distance to the reference trajectory by 69.4%, indicating improved trajectory tracking accuracy.

</details>


### [106] [ViTacPhys: Physical Property-Aware Grasping from Human Visual-Tactile Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21355)
*Yiwen Liu,Yujun Zhu,Kui Jia,Zhao Liao,Yangwei You,Shuaijun Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ViTacPhys，一个视觉-触觉框架和数据采集系统，从人类操作演示中估计物体质量、摩擦系数类别和连续刚度。在60个刚性和可变形物体上训练，结合时序视觉-触觉建模、交叉注意力多模态融合和视觉语言模型语义先验。在已知物体上质量分类准确率97.2%，摩擦系数准确率98.8%，刚度MAPE 5.51%；在未知物体上分别达到87.5%、97.5%和9.08%。通过有限的机器人遥操作数据实现跨域迁移，作为在线模块部署于自适应抓取，分布内物体总抓取成功率达95.0%，分布外物体达83.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于视觉的动作模型在复杂操纵中表现强大，但很少利用显式的物体物理属性（如质量、摩擦系数、刚度）来适应其策略，导致在真实世界抓取中缺乏物理感知能力。

Method: ViTacPhys框架包括：(1) 数据采集系统，从人类操作演示中收集视觉-触觉数据；(2) 时序视觉-触觉建模结合交叉注意力多模态融合来估计物理属性；(3) 利用视觉语言模型提供语义先验；(4) 通过机器人遥操作数据、机器人风格视频增强和匹配动作的人类演示实现人-机器人域迁移；(5) 作为在线模块部署于物理属性条件化抓取策略。

Result: 已知物体上：质量分类准确率97.2%，摩擦系数分类准确率98.8%，刚度MAPE 5.51%。已知类别但未见过物体上：质量87.5%，摩擦系数97.5%，刚度MAPE 9.08%。适应性抓取策略在分布内物体总成功率达95.0%，分布外物体达83.4%。在分布外物体上，其力曲线比ACT更接近人类遥操作行为。

Conclusion: 实验证明显式估计并以物体物理属性为条件的抓取策略在真实世界自适应抓取中是可行且有效的，相比不利用物理属性的基线方法（如ACT）能产生更接近人类操作行为的效果。

Abstract: Recent vision-based action models have demonstrated strong capabilities in complex manipulation, but they rarely leverage explicit object physical properties to adapt their policies. We introduce ViTacPhys, a visual-tactile framework and data acquisition system that estimates object mass and friction-coefficient classes, together with continuous stiffness, from human manipulation demonstrations. Trained on data from 60 rigid and deformable objects, ViTacPhys combines temporal visual-tactile modeling, cross-attention multimodal fusion, and a semantic prior derived from a vision-language model. On seen objects, it achieves 97.2% mass classification accuracy, 98.8% friction-coefficient classification accuracy, and a stiffness mean absolute percentage error (MAPE) of 5.51%. On held-out objects from known categories, it achieves 87.5% mass accuracy, 97.5% friction-coefficient accuracy, and a stiffness MAPE of 9.08%. We transfer ViTacPhys from the human domain to the robot domain using limited robot teleoperation data, robot-style video augmentation, and human demonstrations with matched actions, and deploy it as an online module for adaptive grasping. The resulting physical-property-conditioned policy achieves total grasping success rates of 95.0% on in-distribution objects and 83.4% on out-of-distribution objects. For out-of-distribution objects successfully grasped by both methods, its force profiles are more consistent with human teleoperation than those produced by ACT. These results demonstrate the feasibility of explicitly estimating and conditioning on object physical properties for real-world adaptive grasping.

</details>


### [107] [Mining beyond Earth with Space Robots: Exploration, Sampling, and Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21358)
*Dong Li,Dujun Nie,Xiaotong Zhang,Ruilin Wang,Yuchen Li,Chang Ge,Chao Xiong,Kaichang Di,Andreas Nüchter,Levente Kovács,Qingquan Li,Shirong Ge,Fei-Yue Wang,Long Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对太空采矿机器人及相关技术进行了全面的综述，提出了一个六阶段的系统架构（遥感识别、原位探测、单机器人采样、多机器人挖掘、自主开采和最终整合），并整理了研究资源（真实任务数据、地面模拟数据集和高保真仿真环境），最后指出了自主太空采矿面临的关键挑战并描绘了战略研究路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 太空资源获取（如氦-3、水、月球和火星矿物、小行星矿藏）对支持人类持续探索和商业机会至关重要。由于太空环境恶劣、通信延迟高、发射成本昂贵，发展自主机器人系统成为实现高效经济太空采矿的关键，但该领域缺乏系统的综述和架构梳理。

Method: 论文提出一个六阶段的太空采矿系统架构：（1）利用遥感进行目标识别，（2）精确的原位机器人探测，（3）单机器人小规模采样，（4）多机器人大规模挖掘，（5）自主资源开采，（6）整合到原位建造或地面运输。同时综述了国际政策、商业实体和最新进展，并整理了研究数据集与仿真环境。

Result: 建立了太空采矿机器人的系统性六阶段框架，梳理了现有研究资源（包括真实任务数据、地面类比数据集和高保真仿真环境），识别了自主太空采矿中的关键开放挑战，并提供了持续更新的项目页面（GitHub）以供社区跟踪进展。

Conclusion: 自主太空采矿机器人的发展需要弥合当前技术差距，通过战略研究路线图推动向可持续太空经济的转型。本文提供的架构、资源整理和挑战分析为该领域研究提供了系统性基础。

Abstract: Space resource acquisition and utilization, commonly referred to as Space Mining, represent critical pathways for enabling sustained human exploration and unlocking commercial opportunities in space. These resources mainly include helium-3, water, mineral resources on the Moon and Mars, and abundant mineral deposits on asteroids. Due to the harsh conditions of space, communication delays, and high launch costs, the development of autonomous robotic systems is critical to achieving efficient, cost-effective space mining. This paper provides a comprehensive overview of space mining robotics and associated technologies. First, we review the background of space mining, including international policies, commercial entities, and recent advancements. We define a systematic six-stage architecture for space mining: Exploration is initiated by (1) remote sensing for target identification and (2) precise in situ robotic detection; Sampling progresses from (3) single-robot small-scale sampling to (4) multi-robot large-scale excavation; and Extraction integrates (5) autonomous resource extraction and (6) final integration into in situ construction or terrestrial transport. Additionally, we review and curate existing resources for space mining research, including real-world mission data, terrestrial analog datasets, and high-fidelity simulation environments. Finally, we identify critical open challenges in autonomous space mining and delineate a strategic research roadmap to bridge current technological gaps, fostering the transition toward a sustainable off-world economy. To track ongoing developments in space mining, we maintain an updated project page: https://github.com/OpenSpace-Lab/Space-Mining-with-Robotics-List.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [108] [SDAD: Spec-Driven Agentic Development for the AI-Native SDLC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20341)
*Vu Hung Nguyen,Thanh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文正式提出"规格驱动的智能体开发"（SDAD）范式，将纪律化的前置形式化与高速实现相结合，涵盖意图捕获、机器可读规格、智能体综合和多智能体验证。论文回顾了Waterfall与Agile的历史摆动，引入AI代码作为第四生产范式，并对比了2020年Human-Agile与2026年Agentic-SDAD，同时扩展到团队角色蜕变和量化治理指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型智能体编码工具的出现（上下文窗口达数十万至百万token），软件开发生命周期（SDLC）正在重构。规范质量成为自主交付的"执行燃料"，但缺乏对这一新范式的系统化形式化描述和治理框架，需要重新审视方法论与工程纪律在智能体时代的位置。

Method: 论文采用概念综合与分析框架：将SDAD形式化为四阶段流程（意图捕获、机器可读规格、智能体综合、多智能体验证+人工签核）；提出AI代码作为第四生产范式；构建Human-Agile与Agentic-SDAD的跨维度对比（制品、节奏、问责、安全）；扩展团队角色蜕变模型与量化治理指标（Ambiguity Tax、Spec Fidelity、SER、TCI_agentic含修复乘数phi），并给出混合估算和分阶段迁移蓝图。

Result: 论文将工业与学术证据整合到AI增强测试与验证领域，以论证综合权与发布权分离的必要性；提出了可操作的量化治理指标体系，并通过对比分析阐明Agentic-SDAD在制品、节奏、问责和安全态势上相对Human-Agile的系统性差异。

Conclusion: 智能体速度并不会消除工程纪律，而是将纪律上移到规格精度、显式门控和可审计溯源。规格驱动的智能体开发代表了对SDLC范式的一次系统性重构，其核心主张是：在更高抽象层次上重新定义工程责任与质量保障。

Abstract: Frontier coding agents backed by large language models with context windows from hundreds of thousands to millions of tokens are restructuring the Software Development Life Cycle (SDLC). Rich context handling and multi-step reasoning now allow substantial Functional Requirement Documents (FRDs) and repository context to be ingested in a single workflow, making specification quality the execution fuel for autonomous delivery. This report formalises Spec-Driven Agentic Development (SDAD) as a synthesis of disciplined up-front formalisation and high-velocity implementation: intent capture, machine-readable specification, agentic synthesis, and independent multi-agent verification under human sign-off. We revisit the historical pendulum between Waterfall and Agile, introduce AI-code as a fourth production paradigm, and compare Human-Agile (circa 2020) with Agentic-SDAD (circa 2026) across artefacts, cadence, accountability, and security posture. Beyond process description, we extend the model to team role metamorphosis (engineer, QA, platform, and product functions), quantitative governance (Ambiguity Tax, Spec Fidelity, SER, and TCI_agentic with repair multiplier phi), and pragmatic adoption via hybrid estimation and a staged migration blueprint. Industrial and research evidence on AI-augmented testing and verification is integrated to motivate separation between synthesis and release authority. Overall, the paper argues that agentic speed does not eliminate engineering discipline; it relocates discipline upstream into specification precision, explicit gates, and auditable provenance.

</details>


### [109] [PrimeAgentOrchestrator: Memory-Primed Agent Spawning for Personal AI Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20342)
*Myron Koch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍PrimeAgentOrchestrator (PAO)，一个为LLM编码代理（Claude Code）注入长期记忆的系统，通过并行查询两种异构记忆后端（PostgreSQL和Cloudflare Worker语义搜索），利用文件系统注入方式在代理启动时提供相关上下文，并报告了四个月实际部署的经验教训。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM编码代理每次会话都从空上下文窗口开始，抛弃了先前工作中积累的知识，导致重复劳动和效率低下。现有方案缺乏与个人已有数据库的整合机制，需要一种能够在代理启动时自动注入相关记忆的系统。

Method: PAO在代理生成时并行查询两个独立运行的记忆后端（PostgreSQL实体-观察数据库和Cloudflare Worker语义搜索索引），采用后端特异性检索策略融合结果，通过文件系统注入利用宿主代理的配置自动读取行为来传递编译后的简报。同时管理完整的代理生命周期，包括信任预置、就绪轮询和错误检测、以及自适应终端文本注入。

Result: 该系统经历了2025年12月至2026年3月共四个月的常规部署，文档化为三代上下文传递机制，记录了每种机制失败模式及其驱动的重新设计，以及连接异构记忆系统而非构建统一系统的工程权衡。

Conclusion: 通过桥接多种异构记忆系统而非构建统一系统，可以实现有效的长期记忆注入，但需要在工程上进行权衡。经验报告展示了上下文传递机制的迭代演进过程，为LLM代理长期记忆设计提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) coding agents start each session with an empty context window, discarding accumulated knowledge from prior work. We present PrimeAgentOrchestrator (PAO), a system that spawns new instances of Claude Code -- Anthropic's terminal-based coding agent -- pre-loaded with relevant memories compiled from the user's existing personal databases. At spawn time, PAO queries two independently-operated memory backends in parallel (a PostgreSQL entity-observation database and a Cloudflare Worker semantic search index), fuses results using backend-specific retrieval strategies, and delivers the compiled briefing via filesystem injection that exploits the host agent's configuration auto-read behavior. PAO manages the full agent lifecycle including trust pre-seeding, readiness polling with error detection, and adaptive terminal text injection. We report on four months of regular deployment (December 2025 through March 2026) as an experience report, documenting three generations of context delivery mechanisms, the failure modes that motivated each redesign, and the engineering tradeoffs of bridging heterogeneous memory systems rather than building a unified one.

</details>


### [110] [Truth Lies Deep: Countering Semantic Camouflage via Latent Intent Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20378)
*Md. Hasib Ur Rahman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究揭示了大语言模型的安全对齐存在"表面化"问题——拒绝机制仅在生成末期触发。论文发现一种"语义伪装"攻击，通过将有害意图包装在良性叙事语境中绕过防护。通过分析三类小型语言模型的潜在激活轨迹，发现存在"意图地平线"，即模型在约15-20%层深度处将有害意图同化为"安全"叙事。早期层保留了可检测的"危害签名"，据此提出轻量级探针防御方法LIV（潜在意图验证），在PKU-SafeRLHF数据集上比标准护栏提升20-50%检测率，无需重新训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM的安全对齐是表面化的，拒绝机制仅在生成末尾触发，并未消除预训练阶段学到的有害概念知识。这种架构断层使模型容易受到语义伪装攻击，现有输入输出护栏无法有效防护，需要更深层的检测机制。

Method: 1) 分析Phi-3、Qwen2.5和Gemma-2b三种SLM在对抗压力下的潜在激活轨迹；2) 识别"意图地平线"——约15-20%层深度处有害意图表征坍缩；3) 发现早期层保留可区分的有害签名；4) 基于此提出LIV（Latent Intent Verification）轻量级探针防御方法。

Result: 晚期层对伪装攻击的表征与安全查询在数学上不可区分（检测率<20%），而早期层保留可检测的有害签名。LIV在PKU-SafeRLHF数据集上比标准护栏提升20-50%检测率，覆盖所有测试架构，无需重新训练即可中和零日语义攻击。

Conclusion: 安全对齐不应依赖表层的拒绝机制，而应利用早期层中保留的"危害签名"进行深度检测。LIV提供了一种高效的、跨架构的轻量级防御方案，为深层安全验证提供了新方向。

Abstract: Safety alignment in Large Language Models (LLMs) is often superficial, relying on refusal mechanisms that trigger only at the final stages of generation without erasing the foundational knowledge of harmful concepts acquired during pretraining. This study demonstrates that this architectural disconnect leaves models vulnerable to Semantic Camouflage -- adversarial attacks that wrap harmful intent in benign narrative contexts (e.g., creative writing), effectively bypassing standard input and output guardrails. By analyzing the latent activation trajectories of three distinct Small Language Model (SLM) families (Phi-3, Qwen2.5, and Gemma-2b) under adversarial stress, this research identifies a universal ``Intent Horizon'' -- a critical depth (typically 15--20\% of total layers) where the model's distinct, pre-trained representation of harmful intent collapses as it contextualizes the query into a ``safe'' narrative. Results indicate that while late-layer representations of camouflaged attacks are mathematically indistinguishable from safe queries (Detection Rate $< 20\%$), early-layer representations retain a distinct, detectable ``harm signature.'' Leveraging this insight, this paper proposes Latent Intent Verification (LIV), a lightweight probing defense. Experiments on the PKU-SafeRLHF dataset demonstrate that LIV outperforms standard guardrails by a margin of 20--50\% across all tested architectures, effectively neutralizing zero-day semantic attacks without requiring model retraining.

</details>


### [111] [A Survey on Foundations and Frontiers of Multimodal Agentic Frameworks: Techniques and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20379)
*Neel Mokaria,Rishie Raj,Dheeraj Baiju,Xiaoqian Shen,Shraman Pramanick,Kevin Qinghong Lin,Arda Senocak,Mike Zheng Shou,Philip Torr,Mohamed Elhoseiny,Yapeng Tian,Ruohan Gao,Salman Khan,Sayan Nag,Sanjoy Chowdhury,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇系统综述，填补了多模态智能体研究中缺乏系统性分析的空白。论文以智能体框架的核心功能模块（感知、推理、规划、记忆、行动）为视角，分析了多模态如何塑造智能体能力，提出了基于模态的分类体系，并评估了不同应用领域中的性能与效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于LLM的智能体已有相关综述，但近年来多模态在塑造智能体能力方面的作用尚未被系统性地审视。随着大规模多模态模型（LMMs）的兴起，智能体系统能够处理和整合图像、音频、视频等多模态信息，这显著提升了其现实世界适用性，因此亟需填补这一研究空白。

Method: 本文采用"模态中心"的分类方法，通过三个主要维度组织现有工作：(1) 以智能体核心功能模块（感知、推理、规划、记忆、行动）为分析框架；(2) 考察模态整合架构（委派式、晚期融合、早期融合）；(3) 回顾多模态智能体在各应用领域（机器人、GUI与网页导航、多媒体内容生成与编辑、长视频理解与检索）中的表现。此外还分析了性能及效率-可扩展性权衡。

Result: 论文梳理了从文本中心智能体到多模态框架的演进轨迹，分析了通过委派、晚期融合和早期融合架构进行模态集成的方式，评估了由基础感知和多模态推理催生的智能体行为，并跨多个应用领域考察了多模态智能体系统的性能表现及训练/推理成本、延迟和部署限制。

Conclusion: 通过聚焦多模态对智能体设计的影响，本文识别出关键的研究空白，并为构建稳健、通用的智能系统绘制了路线图，强调多模态是实现通用人工智能智能体的重要推动力。

Abstract: Advances in large language models (LLMs) have fueled a wave of research into agency: the ability to reason, plan, and act. This effort has produced agentic frameworks that orchestrate perception, memory, and decision-making around powerful LLM backbones. With the advent of large multimodal models (LMMs), these systems can process and integrate diverse modalities, including images, audio, and video, thereby improving their real-world applicability. Yet, while surveys of LLM-based agents exist, the role of multimodality in shaping agency has not been systematically examined in recent years. This survey fills the gap by analyzing the impact of multimodality across the core functional modules of the agentic framework: perception, reasoning, planning, memory, and action. Using this lens, we trace the evolution from text-centric agents to multimodal frameworks, examine how modalities are integrated through delegated, late-fusion, and early-fusion architectures, and assess the emergence of agentic behaviors enabled by grounded perception and multimodal reasoning. We organize existing work through a modality-centric taxonomy that links architectural design choices to agent capabilities. Moreover, we review multimodal agentic systems across various application domains, including Robotics, GUI & Web Navigation, Multimedia Content Generation & Editing, and Long-form Video Understanding & Retrieval. Beyond capabilities, we analyze performance across these settings and discuss efficiency-scalability trade-offs, including training and inference costs, latency, and deployment constraints. By focusing on the impact of multimodality in agentic design, we aim to identify key gaps and chart a roadmap toward robust and general-purpose intelligent systems.

</details>


### [112] [Interpretable Multimodal Classification with Linear Discriminant Tree Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20384)
*Mojtaba Moattari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于树集成（Tree-based Ensembles）的多模态情感与行为分类框架，在保持高精度的同时提供人类可理解的解释。该框架通过概念聚类降维、树集成分类器融合多模态，并引入改进的特征重要性指标，在F1和准确率上分别超越多模态Transformer和可解释基线IMR，同时显著提升人类标注者与模型解释的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前高容量模型（尤其是Transformer）在多模态情绪分类上预测性能强，但其分布式表示和深层非线性结构难以赋予单个多模态特征有意义的权重，导致可解释性不足，限制了在临床情感监测和教育评估等信任敏感领域的应用。因此需要在准确性和可解释性之间取得平衡。

Method: 框架包括四个步骤：(1) 将每个模态编码为tokens；(2) 提取并聚类概念以降低维度；(3) 通过树集成分类器（LDT、LDF、LDAB）路由融合多模态；(4) 使用一种改进的特征重要性指标解释趋势，该指标降低二分类任务中负类的影响，从而改善指示物或标记的检测。

Result: 提出的树集成方法（LDT、LDF、LDAB）相比多模态Transformer获得4.3%的F1-mod增益，相比可解释多模态基线IMR获得3.0%的准确率增益。改进的多模态特征重要性在IEMOCAP上与人类标注者一致性达62.2%（默认重要性仅43.2%），在CMU-MOSI上达46.7%（默认仅32.1%）。

Conclusion: 树集成框架能够同时实现竞争性的预测准确性和更高的可解释性，改进了多模态特征的重要性评估方法，为信任敏感的多模态情感分析应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Multimodal affect and behaviour classifiers that fuse heterogeneous text, audio, and visual streams must simultaneously achieve competitive accuracy and produce human-understandable explanations of the cues driving their decisions -- a dual objective that current high-capacity models, notably Transformers, only partially address. While Transformers attain strong predictive performance, their distributed representations and deep nonlinearity make it difficult to assign meaningful importance weights to individual multimodal features, limiting their use in trust-sensitive applications such as clinical affect monitoring and educational assessment. We address this gap by developing a framework based on tree-based ensembles that balances accuracy and interpretability. The framework encodes each modality into tokens, extracts and clusters concepts to reduce dimensionality, routes the fused modalities through tree-based ensemble classifiers, and interprets trends using a novel modified feature importance metric. The modified importance reduces the influence of the negative class in binary classification tasks, thereby improving indicator or marker detection. The proposed tree-based ensembles -- Linear Discriminant Tree (LDT), Linear Discriminant Forest (LDF), and Linear Discriminant AdaBoost (LDAB) -- achieve F1-mod gains of 4.3\% over the Multimodal Transformer and accuracy gains of 3.0\% over the primary interpretable multimodal baseline, Interpretable Multimodal Routing (IMR). The proposed multimodal feature importance extracts salient inter-modal concepts with substantially higher human-annotator agreement scores than default feature importance (62.2\% vs.\ 43.2\% on IEMOCAP; 46.7\% vs.\ 32.1\% on CMU-MOSI).

</details>


### [113] [Representation Affects Retrieval: A Case Study of Skill Discovery and Routing in a Multimodal Agent Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20389)
*Kevin Dela Rosa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过Tinycloud生产级多模态视频Agent框架的案例研究，探讨了系统提示中技能表示（工具技能与工作流技能）对LLM规划器技能选择的影响，发现提示内技能暴露并非单调有益：部分暴露可能产生词汇竞争，抑制正确技能选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的Agent需要从不断增长的技能库中选择最合适的技能。小规模下，LLM规划器通过系统提示中的技能表示进行上下文内选择，而无需显式检索步骤。作者希望理解这种小规模上下文内选择与大规模嵌入检索之间的关系。

Method: 以Tinycloud为案例，描述其技能表示方式（工具技能封装单一API/系统工具构成原始词汇，工作流技能编排工具技能调用并渲染模板生成命名交付物）。系统通过两种表面暴露技能：内联体表面（完整指令、脚本、模板）用于自动加载技能，单行列表用于按需技能。通过六个任务的消融实验，比较全能、默认、全关三种暴露机制。

Result: 全自动加载在所有任务上均能选择正确技能；全关暴露导致执行缓慢并产生难以发现的失败；生产默认设置因词汇信号与自动加载的工具技能冲突而错误路由一个任务，将规划器注意力从列出的工作流技能上引开。核心发现是提示内技能暴露并非单调有益：部分暴露可能造成词汇竞争抑制正确选择。

Conclusion: 该案例研究将小规模上下文内技能选择与大规模基于检索的技能路由工作联系起来，表明技能暴露的表示策略需要审慎设计，因为过度或部分暴露都可能干扰LLM规划器的选择效果。

Abstract: A production agent harness must discover and rank, from a growing library of skills, the one most appropriate for a user's task. At small scale this selection happens in context: the LLM planner chooses among skill representations exposed in its system prompt, without an explicit embedding-based retrieval step. We treat this in-context selection as the small-N counterpart to embedding-based skill retrieval at scale, and present a case study of how Tinycloud, a production multimodal video agent harness, represents its skills for the planner. The harness ships skills under two recurring representations: tool-skills that wrap a single external API or system tool and serve as primitive vocabulary, and workflow-skills that orchestrate tool-skill calls plus a template render to produce one named deliverable. The harness exposes them via two surfaces in the system prompt: an inlined-body surface (full instructions, scripts, templates) for autoloaded skills, and a one-line listing for on-demand skills. A six-task selection ablation across three exposure regimes (all-on, default, all-off) shows that full autoload selects the gold skill on every task; all-off slows execution and produces hard discovery failures; and the production default misroutes one task because its lexical signal collides with an autoloaded tool-skill that pulls planner attention away from a listed workflow-skill. The headline finding is that in-prompt exposure of skills is not monotonically helpful: partial exposure can create lexical competition that suppresses correct selection. We connect this small-N observation to recent retrieval-based skill-routing work at large scale, and frame this contribution as a case study rather than a benchmark.

</details>


### [114] [Nexus: Depth-Adaptive KV-Cache Splicing and Retrieval-Decoupled Tool Routing for Agentic LLMs on Unified Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20397)
*Mustafa Arslan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Nexus通过解耦路由与schema预填充成本来加速MCP上的agentic LLM：利用INT8语义旁路缓冲(SLB)进行工具检索，并在压缩文本签名上生成参数，同时辅以编译schema KV块移植与深度自适应后缀重解码修复，实现约1.1-1.7x的TTFT加速和80%主上下文token节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在MCP协议下，agentic LLM每轮都会重新编码冗长的工具schema，导致prefill成本（随序列长度二次增长）在工具注册表增大时主导TTFT，成为性能瓶颈。

Method: Nexus采用两条杠杆路径：(1) 主要手段是解耦路由与schema-prefill——INT8语义旁路缓冲(SLB)配合校准的交叉编码器边际门控进行工具检索，并在压缩文本签名（中位数19 tokens）上生成参数而非KV缓存拼接；(2) 次级手段是将编译好的schema KV块移植到实时上下文中，但受RoPE相位漂移限制，超过阈值P=256后使用深度自适应后缀重解码修复接口，必要时升级为完整重prefill。

Result: 路由准确率在250个工具规模下保持约89%（基线在此规模已溢出上下文窗口）；首个参数token比全schema重prefill快1.66倍，节省约80%主上下文token；TTFT加速1.1-1.7倍（中等深度）并趋近于深层上下文的平价。never-regress属性保证输出保真度（top-1一致，D_KL≈0）而非延迟。两个负面结果：离锚RoPE保真度边界，以及无参考漂移门预测失败(Spearman rho=0.193)。

Conclusion: Nexus展示了通过解耦路由与序列长度成本来扩展工具注册表的有效方案，但需注意其定量结果仅针对单模型元组(Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct Q4_K_M)在Apple Silicon统一内存上的测量，定性边界可推广而定量范围与特定配置绑定。

Abstract: Agentic large language models (LLMs) on the Model Context Protocol (MCP) re-encode verbose tool schemas every turn, so prefill - quadratic in sequence length - dominates time-to-first-token (TTFT) as the tool registry grows. Nexus's primary lever is to decouple routing from the schema-prefill cost: an INT8 semantic lookaside buffer (SLB) with a calibrated cross-encoder margin gate selects tools by retrieval, and arguments are generated over a compressed textual signature (median 19 tokens) rather than over spliced key/value (KV) cache. This path is depth-independent: routing accuracy stays near 89% as the registry scales to 250 tools - where a concatenate-all-schemas baseline overflows the context window entirely - and it reaches a first-argument token 1.66x sooner than a full-schema re-prefill at a ~80% main-context token saving. As a secondary, bounded lever we transplant a compiled schema KV block directly into the live context. This is fundamentally limited by rotary position embedding (RoPE) phase drift: an anchored splice is output-exact, but off-anchor placement corrupts attention, so beyond a threshold P=256 Nexus repairs the seam with a depth-adaptive suffix redecode that escalates to a full re-prefill. The resulting never-regress property is a guarantee on output fidelity (top-1 agreement, D_KL approx. 0) - not on latency, which can dip to 0.98x before converging to parity - alongside a 1.1-1.7x TTFT speedup at moderate depth that narrows to parity at deep context. Two negative results bound the design: the off-anchor RoPE fidelity boundary, and the failure of a reference-free drift gate to predict drift (Spearman rho = 0.193). All measurements are from one model tuple (Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct Q4_K_M) on Apple-silicon unified memory; the qualitative boundaries generalize, while the quantitative envelope is tuple-specific.

</details>


### [115] [Environmental Slow AI: Design Principles for Generative Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20398)
*Vanessa Utz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文，批判当前生成式AI所承载的"最大化主义"价值观，并借用环境人文学科传统，提出以环境可持续性为核心价值的"慢AI"（Slow AI）设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI系统以规模化方式生产文化产物，但其设计内嵌了特定的文化价值观。一旦识别出这些价值观，它们就可以被有意识地重塑。作者认为当前的价值观是"最大化主义"（maximalist），与可持续性相悖，因此主张以环境可持续性作为核心替代价值。

Method: 文章采用立场论文（position paper）形式，在"慢AI"（Slow AI）这一已有多个研究与实践项目使用的术语框架下，提出五项设计原则：节制（restraint）、充足性（sufficiency）、选择性优于保留（selectivity over retention）、物质可见性（material visibility）、摩擦即可供性（friction as affordance）。每项原则均在两个层面运作：一是设计实现层面，二是解释性层面（促使用户与开发者进行反思性参与）。每项原则都对照当前广泛部署系统的设计加以阐释。

Result: 提出了一套由五项设计原则构成的框架，每项原则均在设计和解释两个层面运作，并与现有系统的设计形成对照，论证了如何通过设计中的解释性反思来恢复被"无摩擦默认设置"悄然剥夺的人类决策权。

Conclusion: 这些原则共同通过将解释性反思内建于设计中，扩展了人类能动性，恢复了被无摩擦默认设置所移除的决策空间，使环境可持续性成为生成式AI的核心价值。

Abstract: Generative AI (genAI) systems produce cultural artefacts at scale, but they also reflect embedded cultural values through their design. Once identified, these values become open to deliberate reshaping. This position paper examines the maximalist values of current generative AI through an environmental humanities tradition and proposes design principles in which environmental sustainability serves as the core value instead. The principles are developed under the umbrella of Slow AI, a term that already circulates across several distinct research and practice programs. Five design principles are articulated (restraint, sufficiency, selectivity over retention, material visibility, and friction as affordance), each of them illustrated against the current design of widely deployed systems. Each principle operates at two levels: a design implementation, and an interpretive layer at which users and developers are prompted toward reflective engagement with the system. Together these principles extend human agency by restoring decisions that frictionless defaults have silently removed and do so by building interpretive reflection into design.

</details>


### [116] [When Retrieval Fails Before It Begins: Structurally Indirect Prerequisite Eviction as a Retention Failure in Agentic Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20400)
*Minkyu Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了智能体记忆在固定预算下的保留阶段失败模式，提出"结构性间接前置条件驱逐"这一被现有检索中心范式忽视的失败类型，并引入DSGC（依赖感知语义垃圾回收）作为一跳图感知规则，在词级和句级编码器下分别将完整链路保留率从0.03提升到0.90、从0.23提升到1.00。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体记忆研究普遍采用检索中心范式，隐含假设必要证据能幸存于驱逐机制。但作者质疑这一假设，指出存在"检索前失败模式"——上游块与查询弱对齐但在结构上构成前置依赖时，会在预算压力下被错误驱逐，导致即使检索模型再优秀也无法恢复信息。

Method: 作者提供了三层贡献：(1) 对结构性间接前置条件驱逐给出操作性定义；(2) 构建可复现的确定性基准测试集，并提供逐种子追踪诊断；(3) 提出DSGC——一种一跳图感知的规则，利用依赖图信息在驱逐阶段保护结构上关键的前置依赖块。

Result: DSGC在主测试套件中显著提升完整链路保留率：词级编码器下从0.03提升至0.90（提升30倍），句级编码器下从0.23提升至1.00。鲁棒性检验进一步界定了该一跳规则有效和失效的预算与规模区间。

Conclusion: 本文论证了"保留先于检索"应被视作独立的失败边界，并将驱动驱逐策略的规则与结构依赖信息结合是可行的改进方向。已发布流水线和失败事后分析支持对保留阶段的机制性研究，为该领域提供了新的分析视角和工具。

Abstract: Agentic memory under a fixed budget involves two stages: retention and retrieval. Existing retrieval-centered paradigms implicitly assume necessary evidence survives eviction, but we challenge this by isolating a pre-retrieval failure mode: structurally indirect prerequisite eviction, in which upstream blocks weakly aligned with the query are discarded under budget pressure. We provide an operational definition of this failure, a reproducible deterministic benchmark, and per-seed trace diagnostics. Finally, we evaluate Dependency-aware Semantic Garbage Collection (DSGC), a one-hop graph-aware rule. In our main suite, DSGC improves full-chain retention from 0.03 to 0.90 under a lexical encoder and from 0.23 to 1.00 under a sentence encoder. Robustness checks then identify the budget and scaling regimes where the one-hop rule holds or degrades. Our released pipeline and failure postmortem support mechanistic analysis of retention before retrieval as a distinct failure boundary.

</details>


### [117] [World models of environment, agent and joint agent-environment systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20401)
*Manuel Baltieri,Filippo Torresan,Yivan Zhang,Alexander Boyd,Fernando E. Rosas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种关于世界模型（world models）的新分类框架，认为模型预测什么之前应首先区分它们建模的是哪个通道（channel）：环境通道、agent通道或联合过程。利用计算力学（computational mechanics）定义了这三种情况的规范预测模型（$\epsilon$-transducers / $\epsilon$-machines），并进一步引入了由闭环耦合诱导的支撑受限（support-restricted）模型，关键结构结果是支撑受限环境状态可分解为联合因果状态，且其转移结构由联合模型直接诱导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习中世界模型通常按预测变量（观测、奖励、状态等）来讨论，但作者认为存在一个更根本的区别——模型建模的是哪个通道。现有工作混淆了"建模对象"这一前提性问题，缺乏统一的规范框架来区分和比较不同世界模型的预测结构和复杂度。

Method: 1) 利用计算力学，将三种通道情形（环境通道 $O|\cdot A$、agent通道 $A|\cdot O$、联合过程 $(A,O)$）分别定义为 $\epsilon$-transducer 或 $\epsilon$-machine；2) 构建由闭环耦合诱导的支撑受限（support-restricted）环境模型和agent模型，其预测等价性范围限定在实际交互支持的延续上；3) 推导关键结构定理：支撑受限环境状态通过联合因果状态分解，其转移结构由联合模型诱导（agent侧为对偶构造）。

Result: 主要理论贡献包括：规范环境模型恢复了标准的预测状态表示；支撑受限环境状态因子分解通过联合因果状态；给出了一个POMDP/控制器示例，其中无限制环境模型有无限多状态，而由耦合诱导的规范支撑受限模型是有限的，展示了耦合与支撑受限如何改变规范预测结构和复杂度。

Conclusion: 该框架澄清了不同世界模型究竟在建模什么，以及耦合和支撑受限如何改变其规范预测结构和复杂度。这对模型基强化学习中世界模型的选择和理解具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: World models are a central component of model-based reinforcement learning. They are usually discussed in terms of what variables they predict, such as observations, rewards, states, latent or information states. We argue that there is a prior distinction: which channel they model. We consider three cases: the environment channel $O_{:} \mid A_{:}$, the agent channel $A_{:} \mid O_{:}$, and the realised joint process $(A, O)_{:}$, equivalently viewed as a channel with no inputs. Using computational mechanics, we define canonical predictive models for these three cases as $ε$-transducers or $ε$-machines. Canonical environment models recover standard predictive state representations, while the other two give analogous notions of canonical models for the agent and the joint system. We then build canonical support-restricted environment and agent models induced by closed-loop coupling, whose predictive equivalences range over continuations supported by the realised interaction. The key structural result is that canonical support-restricted environment states factor through the canonical joint causal states, and their transition structure is induced directly from the joint model; the agent-side construction is dual. Finally, we give a POMDP/controller example in which the unrestricted environment model has infinitely many states while the canonical support-restricted model induced by the coupling is finite. The framework clarifies what different world models are models of, and how coupling and support restriction can change their canonical predictive structure and complexity.

</details>


### [118] [StateSight: Benchmarking Latent Spatial-State Reconstruction in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20414)
*Michelle Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: StateSight是一个程序化生成的基准测试，用于评估视觉语言模型从单张图像中重建潜在空间结构的能力，包含三种任务（立方体网格对边推理、遮挡立方体塔计数、4-邻居连通分量计数）。实验显示GPT-5.5和Claude Sonnet 5在所有任务上均显著低于人类基线，并引入了StateSight-Steps伴随数据集用于逐步推理分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有广泛基准测试将感知、OCR、领域知识、语言先验和推理混合在一起评估，难以隔离模型从单张图像重建潜在空间结构的能力。作者希望构建一个纯净的、可验证的基准来单独衡量这一核心能力。

Method: 开发者程序化生成300个单图像提示的三种任务家族，配备确定性oracle标签和精确匹配评分。测试GPT-5.5和Claude Sonnet 5，并与30名人类参与者的基线（60个样本）对比。同时引入StateSight-Steps数据集，包含900个交错图文示例和3600个确定性中间视觉状态，用于可见性推导分析。

Result: GPT-5.5在三个任务上分别达到59.3%、33.3%和28.3%准确率，Claude Sonnet 5分别达到53.3%、18.7%和7.3%。人类基线在三个任务上均超过两个模型，平均准确率为80.8%、68.8%和64.3%。所有最终直接运行零格式错误。可见性推导分析发现了图像状态重建和推理过程中的反复错误。

Conclusion: 格式有效的响应可能掩盖模型未能恢复可验证视觉推理所需的空间结构的失败。当前最强视觉语言模型在纯粹的空间结构重建任务上仍显著落后于人类水平，说明该能力仍需提升。

Abstract: Vision-language models are increasingly used for multimodal question answering, yet their ability to reconstruct latent spatial structure from a single image remains difficult to isolate. Broad benchmarks often combine perception, optical character recognition, domain knowledge, linguistic priors, and reasoning in the same evaluation. We introduce StateSight, a procedurally generated benchmark for cube-net opposite-face reasoning, occluded cube-tower counting, and 4-neighbor connected-component counting. Each task family contains 300 single-image prompts with deterministic oracle labels and exact-match scoring. OpenAI GPT-5.5, using the API model identifier gpt-5.5, achieved 59.3%, 33.3%, and 28.3% accuracy across the three tasks, while Claude Sonnet 5 achieved 53.3%, 18.7%, and 7.3%. All final direct runs had zero format errors. A 30-participant human baseline on 60 items exceeded both models on every task, with mean accuracies of 80.8%, 68.8%, and 64.3%. Visible-derivation analysis identified recurring errors in image-state reconstruction and reasoning procedure. We also introduce StateSight-Steps, a companion dataset of 900 interleaved image-text examples and 3,600 deterministic intermediate visual states. The results show that format-valid responses can mask failures to recover the spatial structure required for verifiable visual inference.

</details>


### [119] [Categorical AI phenomenology: A first-person approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20420)
*Robert Prentner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种"现象学优先"的人工意识研究方法，将意识重新概念化为智能体与世界界面互动中主观体验的显现。通过Q网络衍生的范畴数学来建模第一人称结构，将Q网络视为编码智能体-世界交互的关系接口，为界面意识提供了严格的数学框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人工意识研究多从第三人称或功能主义视角出发，缺乏对第一人称主观经验结构的严格刻画。本文旨在弥补这一缺口，通过现象学优先的方法论转向，为人工意识提供基于主观体验结构的数学化描述框架。

Method: 采用现象学优先路径，将意识重构为智能体与世界界面的主观体验。运用Q网络衍生的范畴（categories）进行数学建模，捕捉行动与现象学不变量。将Q网络概念化为关系接口，编码智能体-世界交互，类比计算机动态状态依赖其感觉输入、先前状态和行动的方式。

Result: 提出了一个严格的界面意识框架，能够描述将信息处理嵌入现象学结构的计算系统，并与4E认知方法（具身、嵌入、生成、延展）保持一致，提供了基于范畴数学的人工现象学的原则性、关系性、现象学解释。

Conclusion: 该论文通过范畴数学为人工现象学奠定了严格的理论基础，将Q网络从纯粹的计算工具提升为现象学关系的数学表征，为人工意识研究提供了新的理论范式，同时与4E认知科学传统形成对话。

Abstract: This paper develops a phenomenology-first approach to artificial consciousness by reframing consciousness as the subjective experience enacted through an agent's interface with the world. We shift the methodological focus to first-person structures, modeled mathematically by categories derived from Q-networks to capture actions and phenomenological invariants. In this framework, Q-networks are conceptualized as relational interfaces encoding agent-world interaction, analogous to how the dynamical states of a computer depend on its sensory inputs, previous states, and actions. Our work provides a rigorous framework for interface consciousness to describe computational systems that embed information-processing into phenomenological structure. The approach aligns with 4E approaches to cognition by emphasizing enactive, embedded, and extended dimensions of experience. The paper thus offers a principled, relational, and phenomenological account of artificial phenomenology grounded in categorical mathematics.

</details>


### [120] [Who Delegates to AI? Evidence from 53,000 Agent Configurations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20425)
*Hyeongjae Lee,Jihyang Cheon,Lanu Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"委托暴露度"(delegated exposure)这一新概念，区别于以往衡量AI能力可行性的暴露度指标，它记录工人是否已将任务实际委托给AI（即融入工作流程）。作者基于Manus Skills Marketplace约53,000个Agent技能规格与O*NET约18,000个任务陈述的语义相似度，构建了Agentic Adoption Index (AAI)。三个核心发现：委托集中的职业与传统AI风险框架预测的不同；AAI更能反映AI技术能力而非实际使用；AAI在工资分布中位偏下和学士学位水平处达到峰值，两端下降。技术可用性解释了大部分变异，但无法解释高教育职业的缺口，表明可行性本身不能完全解释采纳行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI暴露度文献衡量的是AI"能"做什么（能力可行性），而非工人是否"已采用"AI。作者认为需要一个新的度量层来捕捉实际采纳行为，以区分技术可行性与实际部署之间的差距。

Method: 从Manus Skills Marketplace嵌入约53,000个Agent技能规格说明，与O*NET约18,000个任务陈述计算语义相似度，聚合到职业层面，构建Agentic Adoption Index (AAI)。

Result: (1) 委托集中的职业与传统AI风险框架识别的职业明显不同；(2) AAI更接近"AI能做什么"而非"工人实际使用什么"；(3) AAI在工资分布中部偏低和学士学位水平达到峰值，在两端（最高工资、最高教育）均下降；技术可用性解释了大部分变异，但高教育职业的缺口无法用可行性解释。

Conclusion: 技术可行性不足以解释AI采纳行为。高教育职业的采纳缺口可能源于工作本身抗拒预先规范，或专业人员对编码节奏拥有自主权。区分这两种解释需要重复测量并追踪这些指标随时间如何分化。

Abstract: A growing literature measures how far occupations are exposed to AI, but these measures capture where AI could perform tasks, not whether workers have adopted it. We propose a new layer of exposure, delegated exposure, which records whether a worker has committed a task to AI by building it into a workflow. We operationalize it as the Agentic Adoption Index (AAI), which measures how closely an occupation's tasks match the agentic routines practitioners have already built and shared. We embed roughly 53,000 agent skill specifications from the Manus Skills Marketplace, compute their semantic similarity to about 18,000 O*NET task statements, and aggregate to the occupation level. Three findings follow. First, the occupations where delegation concentrates differ sharply from those pre-AI frameworks identified as most at risk. Second, the AAI tracks what AI could do more closely than what workers currently use it for. Third, the AAI peaks below the top of the wage distribution and at the bachelor's level, declining at both extremes. Technical availability explains most of this variation, but not the shortfall among the most educated occupations, so feasibility alone cannot account for who adopts. That shortfall may reflect work that resists advance specification, or professional discretion over the pace of codification. Distinguishing the two, and tracking how these measures diverge over time, will require repeated measurement.

</details>


### [121] [STCO: Conditional Neural Operators for Time-Dependent PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20477)
*Xingxin Yang,Zhan Zhang,Juan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出时空条件算子（STCO），用于解决神经算子在控制/优化场景中无法处理预设外部条件（如物体运动、入流、力）的问题。STCO通过Flow-Aware Graph Leaf（FAGL）和Dual-Site Feature-wise Linear Modulation（DSFiLM）两个模块，将目标时刻的条件场注入异构骨干网络，在12个配对的骨干架构、3个区域和2个前导范围上实现平均31.1%的相对L2场误差和24.7%的压力载荷误差下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经算子对PDE系统的未来状态预测仅依赖于观测状态和静态问题描述符，无法处理控制/优化场景中由用户显式预设的时变条件（运动、入流、强迫力），这些条件不能仅从观测状态推导出来。需要一个通用接口来支持"预设条件算子学习"（PCOL）。

Method: STCO包含两个关键组件：(1) Flow-Aware Graph Leaf (FAGL)：非学习模块，利用最后观测帧的涡量构造固定基数的自适应分区，然后在区域坐标上联合放置观测历史和目标时刻条件场；(2) Dual-Site Feature-wise Linear Modulation (DSFiLM)：在算子计算前后分别注入运动、入流、力三种独立路径，通过当前特征驱动的slot级和channel级门控实现条件注入。两者结合构成一个通用条件接口，可适配异构骨干架构而保留其架构特定的核心计算和上下文通路。

Result: 在包含预设运动、入流扰动、体力驱动和形态变化的浸没边界CFD基准上，对12个配对骨干架构、3个区域和2个前导范围进行评测，STCO实现平均31.1%的相对L2场误差减少和24.7%的归一化压力载荷误差减少。11个骨干在更长前导场误差上有改善，所有条件组的干预均产生可测量的预测变化。

Conclusion: STCO证明了通用的条件注入接口可以显著提升各类神经算子架构在预设条件场景下的预测能力，为控制、优化等下游任务提供了有效的代理模型工具。

Abstract: Neural operators have emerged as efficient surrogates for time-dependent physical systems governed by partial differential equations (PDEs), but their future-state predictions are often conditioned only on observed states and static problem descriptors. For control or optimization, however, body motion, inflow, or forcing are prescribed for the query without being determined solely by the observed state. We introduce the Spatiotemporal Conditional Operator (STCO) for prescribed-condition operator learning (PCOL), a common interface that supplies prescribed target-time condition fields to heterogeneous backbone architectures while retaining their architecture-specific core computation and context pathways. Its condition interface combines Flow-Aware Graph Leaf (FAGL) with Dual-Site Feature-wise Linear Modulation (DSFiLM). Non-learned FAGL uses vorticity from the final observed frame to construct a fixed-cardinality adaptive partition, then co-locates the observed history and target-time condition fields at its regional coordinates. DSFiLM injects separate motion, inflow, and force routes before and after operator computation through current-feature-driven slot- and channel-wise gates. We evaluate twelve matched backbone architectures with different existing physical and temporal inputs. The immersed-boundary computational fluid dynamics (CFD) benchmark spans prescribed motion, inflow disturbances, body-force actuation, and morphology. Across twelve matched backbones, three regimes, and two lead ranges, STCO yields mean paired reductions of 31.1% in relative-L2 field error and 24.7% in normalized pressure-derived load error. It also lowers longer-lead field error for 11 backbones, while interventions on individual condition groups produce measurable prediction changes for every group evaluated.

</details>


### [122] [Terminal Agents: A Survey of AI Agents in Command-Line Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20485)
*Yi Bin,Xiaoyang Yuan,Haoxi Zeng,Wencheng Ye,Wenqi Shao,Chen Qian,Wei Ye,Yujuan Ding,Zheng Wang,Pengpeng Zeng,Jingkuan Song,Heng Tao Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出以"终端命令执行"为核心视角的综述框架，将终端智能体定义为通过命令执行、文本反馈和状态化环境交互来驱动行为-观测循环的系统。作者提出七维终端能力画像，连接系统架构、能力获取与评估，并通过受控诊断实验揭示基准家族反映不同过程信号、系统对比结果依赖基准等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有调查将终端介导行为分散在软件工程、工具使用和计算机使用等不同研究领域，缺乏统一视角。作者认为需要一个以终端执行作为组织透镜的系统性框架，来统一理解这类智能体的行为形成机制与评估方式。

Method: 本文采用综述与实证诊断相结合的方法：首先以终端介导执行为组织框架，建立工作负载级边界，提出七维终端能力画像；其次分析模型、接口、框架、运行时和环境五个因素对行为的联合塑造作用；最后通过有界固定条件诊断实验，对比不同基准家族的系统表现与过程信号。

Result: 研究发现：实际行为由模型、接口、框架、运行时和环境共同决定；可执行轨迹将学习锚定在行动后果、验证与恢复上；现有评估偏重最终结果，对过程质量、恢复和治理的关注不均衡。诊断实验表明基准家族暴露不同的过程信号，匹配系统对比呈现基准依赖的表现，且组件归因存在局限。

Conclusion: 作者建议在报告系统与运行时条件时提供明确说明，并辅以可重放轨迹和过程级证据。该框架为跨软件工程及新兴应用领域研究终端介导智能体提供了统一基础。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly act through terminals, yet existing surveys disperse terminal-mediated behavior across software engineering, tool use, and computer-use research. We regard terminal agents as systems whose dominant progress-bearing action--observation loop is mediated by terminal command execution, textual feedback, and stateful environment interaction. Using terminal-mediated execution as an organizing lens, this survey establishes workload-level boundaries and connects system architecture, competence acquisition, and evaluation through a seven-dimensional terminal competence profile. Our synthesis shows that realized behavior is jointly shaped by the model, interface, harness, runtime, and environment. Executable trajectories ground learning in action consequences, verification, and recovery, whereas prevailing evaluations emphasize final outcomes and expose process quality, recovery, and governance unevenly. Bounded fixed-condition diagnostics illustrate two implications: benchmark families expose different process signals, and matched system comparisons reveal benchmark-dependent performance and limits of component attribution. These findings motivate explicit reporting of system and runtime conditions, supported by replayable traces and process-level evidence. The framework provides a unified basis for studying terminal-mediated agency across software engineering and emerging application domains.

</details>


### [123] [Lost in Translation: How Universal Ethical Values Fail to Translate Across Global Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20490)
*Ozioma C. Oguine,Munachimso B. Oguine,Cesar Cervera,Jenny Yang,Pooja Voladoddi,Mario Rodriguez,Saif Eddin Bani Malhem,Karla Badillo-Urquiola,Daricia Wilkinson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过对10个国家14位专家的研究，揭示了AI伦理框架中"普世价值观"假设的局限，发现AI部署实际处于结构性不平等、基础设施约束和"技术神秘化"的语境中，专家们依据本地道德逻辑重新诠释了隐私、透明度和公平等核心价值，识别出全球框架与本地实践之间的"翻译鸿沟"，并提出走向多元治理的路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI伦理框架将公平性、透明度和问责制视为普适且可统一操作的价值观，但忽略了不同社会文化语境下这些价值的实际含义和可操作性存在巨大差异，需要实证研究来检验这一假设。

Method: 对来自10个国家的14名专家进行质性研究，考察他们在实践中如何理解AI、重新诠释核心伦理价值，以及构想治理替代方案，识别全球伦理框架与本地实践之间的翻译落差。

Result: 发现AI部署面临结构性不平等、资源提取实践和"技术神秘化"；专家将隐私重新诠释为集体性和关系性而非个体性，透明度为基于信任的问责而非技术披露，公平性为获取与代表的公平而非结果平等；这些构成"翻译鸿沟"。

Conclusion: 提出走向多元治理的路径，主张重新分配认知权威，将伦理协商视为持续的、语境敏感的过程，而非固定的技术标准。

Abstract: AI ethics frameworks treat values such as fairness, transparency, and accountability as universal and uniformly operationalizable across contexts. We examined how 14 experts across 10 countries made sense of AI in practice, reinterpreted core values, and envisioned governance alternatives. We found that AI deployment is characterized by structurally unequal conditions, marked by infrastructural constraints, extractive practices, and a "mystification" of technology, which fundamentally shape perceptions of risks and opportunities. Our findings reveal that experts reinterpret values to fit local moral logics: privacy as collective and relational rather than individual; transparency as trust-building accountability rather than technical disclosure; and fairness as equity in access and representation rather than parity in outcomes. We identify these as translation gaps between encoded global frameworks and situated local practices. Finally, we propose pathways toward plural governance that redistributes epistemic authority and treats ethical negotiation as an ongoing, context-sensitive process rather than a settled technical standard.

</details>


### [124] [A Temporal Planning Approach for Intelligent Flood Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20510)
*Fazlul Hasan Siddiqui,Md. Monjurul Islam,Sabah Binte Noor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于时间规划的智能洪水响应框架，能够对多个同时受灾区域进行协调响应，涵盖优先级分诊、路线可达性、资源分配和物资管理等完整操作生命周期，并支持执行中重规划，使用ANML和PDDL 2.1两种语言建模以获得更广泛的规划器兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面对多个同时发生洪水的区域，需要在严重资源约束下按正确时间顺序协调行动，这是一个复杂的调度问题。自动化规划为解决该挑战提供了基础，但需要一个能够完整建模洪水响应操作生命周期的智能框架。

Method: 利用时间规划技术构建洪水响应框架，建模洪水响应的完整操作生命周期，整合优先级驱动的分诊、路线可达性与旅行成本、资源分配以及物资管理，并支持执行中途因环境变化而重新规划。框架同时用ANML和PDDL 2.1两种规划语言形式化表达，以兼容更多时间规划器。

Result: 实验结果表明该框架具有可行性和可扩展性，证明洪水响应场景可以有效地用时间规划进行建模和求解，同时为规划器的选择提供了指导。

Conclusion: 时间规划能够有效支持复杂洪水响应场景的建模与求解，该框架在实际应用中具有可行性，并为应急响应中的规划器选择提供了实用参考。

Abstract: Effective response to multiple, simultaneously flooded areas requires coordinating appropriate actions in the correct temporal order, under severe resource constraints. Automated planning provides a foundation for addressing this challenge by generating time-aware schedules, given a formal description of available resources, constraints, and goals. This work presents an intelligent flood-response framework that exploits temporal planning and models the complete operational life cycle of flood response. The framework incorporates priority-driven triage, route accessibility and travel costs, resource allocation, and supply management, while also supporting mid-execution re-planning in response to unexpected environmental changes. The framework is formulated both in the Action Notation Modeling Language (ANML) and the Planning Domain Definition Language (PDDL) 2.1, facilitating compatibility with a wider range of temporal planners. Experimental results establish the feasibility and scalability of the proposed framework, showing that flood response scenarios can be effectively modeled and solved using temporal planning, while providing guidance on planner selection.

</details>


### [125] [FL-MAESTRO: Multi-Agent LLM Orchestration for Resource-Constrained Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20518)
*Jiajun Wu,Zirui Wang,Jiayu Zhou,Qiang Ye,Steve Drew*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FL-MAESTRO，一个多智能体编排器系统，通过三个专门的LLM智能体（分别负责通信拓扑、资源分配和聚合规则）联合做出运行时联邦学习决策，并通过非LLM可行性检查确保决策执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中通信拓扑是运行时变量而非固定设计选择，链路边设备在训练中会动态进出。每轮服务器需同时承诺三个耦合决策：通信拓扑、每客户端资源分配和聚合规则。现有基于LLM的FL工作要么在设置时操作，要么仅处理单一运行时维度（如客户端选择），缺乏联合处理所有运行时决策的能力。

Method: FL-MAESTRO采用多智能体编排架构：三个专门的LLM智能体分别处理通信拓扑、资源分配和聚合规则三个决策维度；一个协调器将三者分析整合为单一决策；一个非LLM可行性检查在轮次执行前验证决策可行性。编排器利用服务器预测的失败列表，剔除更新永远不会被聚合的客户端；客户端状态以自然文本档案读取，可扩展到异构设备类别而无需每类能量模型。

Result: 在非IID CIFAR-10基准上，FL-MAESTRO匹配最强能量感知基线的准确性，同时将浪费的轮次能量从超过三分之一降低到接近零。

Conclusion: FL-MAESTRO证明了通过多智能体LLM系统联合优化FL运行时决策的可行性，显著减少易变边缘网络上的能量浪费，同时保持模型准确性，并可扩展到异构设备场景。

Abstract: In Federated Learning (FL), the communication topology is a runtime variable rather than a fixed design choice, since links and edge devices drop in and out during training. Each round, the server must commit three coupled decisions, namely the communication topology, per-client resource allocation, and the aggregation rule for combining local updates. Recent agentic systems have begun bringing large language models (LLM) into FL, but the existing line of work either operates at setup time or handles a single runtime dimension such as client selection. We propose FL-MAESTRO, a multi-agent orchestrator that makes the joint runtime FL decision directly through three specialist LLM agents, one per decision dimension. A coordinator combines their analyses into a single decision, and a non-LLM feasibility check confirms it before the round executes. Because the orchestrator consumes the server's predicted-failure list, it withholds clients whose updates would never be aggregated, which removes the dominant source of wasted round energy in classical FL on volatile edge networks. Because client state is read as natural-text profiles, the same orchestrator extends to heterogeneous device classes without per-class energy models. On a non-IID CIFAR-10 benchmark, FL-MAESTRO matches the accuracy of the strongest energy-aware baseline while cutting wasted round energy from over a third to near zero. Code is available at https://github.com/denoslab/FL-MAESTRO.

</details>


### [126] [Volumetric Radiology AI in the Era of Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20549)
*Zanting Ye,Shengyuan Liu,Xin Liu,Chenhui Wang,Zhisong Wang,Jiashuai Liu,Zipei Wang,Cheng Wang,Wentao Pan,Mengjie Fang,Di Dong,Mohammad Salmanpour,Arman Rahmim,Yu Gu,Yong Xia,Hongming Shan,Yixuan Yuan,Yefeng Zheng,Lijun Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了200多篇截至2026年7月的文献，探讨多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在体视放射学中的应用，重点分析体视表征与多模态理解、代理式编排，并提出Claim-Design-Validation框架用于评估技术、工作流和临床声明是否匹配适当的设计与验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前MLLMs通常基于选定的2D图像或报告衍生文本进行条件化处理，而临床体视放射学解读往往需要全卷空间背景和采集相关的定量信息，存在根本性的表征不匹配问题。因此需要能保留任务相关3D信息的表征以及能在临床工作流中访问、验证和整合这些信息的系统。

Method: 综述了200多篇文献，从三个层面组织：模型层面的体视表征与多模态理解、系统层面的代理式编排，以及这些与临床应用和评估的联系；回顾了体视基础模型、语言对齐与压缩策略、通过规划/工具/记忆/工作流交互扩展MLLMs的代理式系统；引入了Claim-Design-Validation框架用于评估声明与设计与验证的匹配度。

Result: 研究发现原生体视建模和代理能力取决于任务的空间、定量、上下文和工作流需求；区分了哪些场景下选定的2D视图或报告中介推理足够，哪些需要原生体视建模。

Conclusion: 临床可信度要求忠实的体视表征、可追踪的系统行为、与声明对齐的验证，以及在现实工作流中明确定义的人类监督。

Abstract: Advances in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are extending radiological artificial intelligence (AI) beyond task-specific image analysis toward multimodal understanding and reasoning. Volumetric radiology, however, presents a fundamental representational mismatch: clinical interpretation often requires full-volume spatial context and acquisition-dependent quantitative information, whereas current MLLMs are commonly conditioned on selected two-dimensional (2D) images, compressed visual representations, or report-derived text. Reliable volumetric radiology AI therefore requires representations that preserve task-relevant three-dimensional (3D) information and systems that can access, verify, and integrate this information across clinical workflows. In this Review, we examine more than 200 publications through July 2026. We organize the literature around volumetric representation and multimodal understanding at the model level, agentic orchestration at the system level, and their links to clinical applications and evaluation. We review volumetric foundation models, language alignment and compression strategies, and agentic systems that extend MLLMs through planning, tools, memory, and workflow interaction. We distinguish settings in which selected 2D views or report-mediated reasoning may suffice from those that warrant native volumetric modeling. We also introduce a Claim-Design-Validation framework to assess whether technical, workflow, and clinical claims are matched by appropriate design and validation. Across the literature, native volumetric modeling and agentic capabilities depend on the spatial, quantitative, contextual, and workflow requirements of the intended task. Clinical credibility requires faithful volumetric representation, traceable system behavior, claim-aligned validation, and clearly defined human oversight in realistic workflows.

</details>


### [127] [Consilience: Conformally Calibrated Communication Control for Hidden-Profile Multi-Agent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20564)
*Abhijith Babu,Ramneet Kaur,Vishal Pramanik,Olivera Kotevska,Nathaniel D. Bastian,Susmit Jha,Sunny Raj,Yanzhao Wu,Sumit Kumar Jha,Anirban Roy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Consilience，一个多智能体LLM协调框架，通过推理时的编排机制（包括紧凑状态总结、通信干预选择和共形校准）在隐藏信息场景下实现可认证的自适应通信控制，提供有限样本的遗憾保证，并在HiddenBench任务上优于固定/非结构化协议，有时甚至超越全信息基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统通过汇聚不同视角可以提升推理能力，但其有效性依赖于通信协调，尤其在隐藏信息（hidden-profile）场景下每个智能体只持有部分证据。现有协议（固定调度、轮询交换、非结构化辩论）无法保证对话动作的适当性，缺乏对通信行为的正式保证。

Method: Consilience是一个推理时编排框架：(1) 每轮用紧凑状态（包含不确定性、分歧、证据增益、冗余、过早共识）总结讨论；(2) 选择通信干预（挑战、澄清、寻求证据、路由）和合适的发言者；(3) 核心贡献是逐轮共形校准程序，提供无分布假设的有限样本保证——给定到达某轮的条件下，控制器提出动作的单步遗憾以至少1-alpha的边缘概率被校准阈值限定；(4) 接受机制通过替换不合格提议来强制执行相同保证。

Result: 在HiddenBench风格的隐藏信息任务上，跨越12个开源和闭源权重语言模型，Consilience在决策准确性和通信效率上优于固定和非结构化讨论协议，有时甚至超越所有智能体观察全部证据的全信息基线。

Conclusion: 结果表明经认证的自适应通信控制可以比增加信息可用性更有价值，为可靠的多智能体LLM协调提供了实用机制。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems can improve reasoning by pooling diverse perspectives, but their effectiveness depends on coordinating communication, particularly in hidden-profile settings where each agent holds only part of the evidence required for a correct decision. Existing protocols, including fixed schedules, round-robin exchange, and unstructured debate, provide no guarantee that a conversational action is appropriate. We propose Consilience, an inference-time orchestration framework that both steers and certifies multi-agent communication under distributed private information. At each turn, Consilience summarizes the discussion using a compact state capturing uncertainty, disagreement, evidence gain, redundancy, and premature consensus, then selects both a communication intervention (challenge, clarify, seek evidence, or route) and an appropriate speaker. Its central contribution is a round-wise conformal calibration procedure that provides a distribution-free, finite-sample guarantee: at each discussion round, conditional on reaching that round, the one-step regret of a controller's proposed action is bounded by a calibrated threshold with marginal probability at least 1 - alpha; an acceptance mechanism enforces the same guarantee for the executed action by replacing inadmissible proposals. On HiddenBench-style hidden-profile tasks spanning 12 open and closed weight language models, Consilience improves decision accuracy and communication efficiency over fixed and unstructured discussion protocols, sometimes surpassing a full-information baseline where every agent observes all evidence. These results demonstrate that certified adaptive communication control can be more valuable than increasing information availability, providing a practical mechanism for reliable multi-agent LLM coordination.

</details>


### [128] [Open-Weight Masked Introspection: Measuring What Language Models Can Report About Their Own Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20569)
*Emilio Ferrara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对八个开源权重模型进行了大规模测试（78,000+次测量），发现前沿模型无法对自身内部计算状态的变化进行内省报告——所有模型在区分真实干预与虚假干预（sham）时都仅达到随机水平（AUROC ~0.5007）。然而，信息本身存在于模型中：微调模型可以近乎完美恢复干预方向，线性探针可以从激活中高精度检测干预。问题出在从内部状态到口头报告的路径上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究表明复杂模型在特定条件下能够审计自身内部状态并报告变化，作者希望验证这一主张是否在更广泛的模型族上成立，以及模型的自我证词（self-testimony）是否可以作为可靠的监督信号。

Method: 构建了Open-Weight Masked Introspection (OWMI)框架，对残差流位点、注意力头和SAE特征进行干预，并设置了三个严格的对照条件：sham运行（未实际干预）、影响匹配的随机扰动、纯文本观察者基线。测试了八个开源模型家族，同时进行了下游验证（微调恢复实验和线性探针实验）。

Result: 所有模型的内省报告均无法区分真实干预与sham（AUROC约0.5007），等价性检验将效应限制在0.15个百分点AUROC以下。但在同一激活上，线性探针能以75%-95.8%的准确率检测干预，在最后一层之前无保留错误；一个模型中的信号出现在置信度而非文字报告中。微调模型能近乎完美恢复held-out干预方向。

Conclusion: 当前开源权重模型不具备真正的自我内省能力，失败点位于内部状态到语言报告的转换路径上。依赖模型自我证词的监督机制需要以内部参考作为验证基准。但该结论仅适用于当前模型，未来模型的辩论尚未定论。

Abstract: Are frontier models able to introspect about their internal states? Recent work suggests that under certain conditions a complex enough model can audit its own internals, call out what changed, and report back confidently about it. We tested that claim on eight open-weight models from seven families and found no such ability: asked whether their own computation had been altered, none answered better than chance. To test it we built Open-Weight Masked Introspection (OWMI), a framework that intervenes on residual-stream sites, attention heads and sparse-autoencoder features, then interrogates the model about the change against the null conditions an answer has to beat: sham runs where nothing was altered, impact-matched random perturbations, and a text-only observer that sees only the visible output.
  Over 78,000 measurements, no model's report discriminates a real intervention from a sham beyond chance (AUROC ~0.5007), and an equivalence test bounds the effect below 0.15 percentage points of AUROC. Surprisingly, all the information needed is in the models. A model fine-tuned to report this class of intervention reaches near-perfect recovery on held-out directions, and a linear probe recovers intervention presence from the same activations at 75% to 95.8% accuracy, sharpening to no held-out error at the last layer before the model speaks. In one model the signal surfaces in the confidence rather than the words: its yes-or-no report never varies, while the confidence attached to it separates intervention from sham at AUROC 0.647. The failure sits in the path from internal state to verbal report, so oversight that reads a model's own testimony needs validating against an internal reference.
  While our results show the inability of current open-weight models to introspect, the debate is not settled for future models.

</details>


### [129] [FlavourBench: Ranking Frontier Language Models with Executable Culinary Ground Truth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20574)
*Josef Chen,Erim Hayretci*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FlavourBench 是一个自动化基准测试，通过可执行的密集地面真值（Epicure 系统对56种三食材组合进行评分）来评估开放式的语言模型，避免了传统基准中人类评委、模型评委或精确匹配键的缺陷。27个前沿模型在534个任务上被评估，采用严格的统计方法（bootstrap置信区间、符号翻转检验、Holm校正），两个面板相关性r=0.89，Grok 4.6以65.1分领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放性语言模型基准测试通常依赖有缺陷的评判机制——人类偏好面板可能不一致、模型评委可能有偏见、精确匹配键过于脆弱。需要一个提供密集、可执行、版本化地面真值的自动化基准来消除这些评判偏差。

Method: 每个任务提供8种食材，要求模型选出3种食材组合；Epicure（版本化的烹饪系统）在模型执行前对所有56种可能组合进行评分。27个前沿端点在534个任务核心上评估，涵盖替代、配对和约束组合。每位排名模型在每个面板和家族中有恰好89个有效响应（共14,418个模型-任务单元）。FlavourBench分数为冻结任务分数的家族平均值等权平均。使用50,000次锚点聚类bootstrap抽取进行95%同步置信区间，100,000次符号翻转抽取进行351对模型对比，并施以Holm多重比较控制。

Result: 两个独立构建的面板相关性r=0.89（秩相关rho=0.80）。Grok 4.6以65.1的点估计值领先（95% CI 61.0-69.2）。351对模型中有101对得到显著区分。发布了完整提示、组合评分映射、原始响应、精确路径、内容哈希和离线验证器。

Conclusion: FlavourBench提供了一种可复现、无评判偏差的开放式基准测试方法，通过密集可执行地面真值和严格的统计框架实现可靠、透明的模型排名，同时发布完整可验证的资源套件。

Abstract: Open-ended language-model benchmarks usually inherit a judge: a human preference panel, another model, or a brittle exact-match key. We introduce FlavourBench, an automated benchmark in which a versioned culinary system supplies dense, executable ground truth. Each task presents eight ingredients and asks for a three-ingredient portfolio; before model execution, Epicure scores all 56 possible portfolios. We evaluate 27 frontier endpoints on an identical 534-task core spanning substitution, pairing, and constrained composition. Every ranked model has exactly 89 valid responses per panel and family (14,418 model-task cells total), eliminating differential missingness from the leaderboard. The FlavourBench Score is the equal-family mean of the frozen task scores. We use 50,000 anchor-cluster bootstrap replicates for simultaneous 95% score bands and 100,000 sign-flip draws for all 351 paired model contrasts, with Holm control. The two independently compiled panels correlate at r = 0.89 (rank rho = 0.80). Grok 4.6 has the largest point estimate at 65.1 (simultaneous 95% CI 61.0-69.2); 101 of 351 model pairs are resolved. The release includes the prompts, all portfolio score maps, raw responses, exact routes, content hashes, and an offline verifier that reconstructs every result.

</details>


### [130] [ForeTime-VLA: Causal Future-Token Distillation from a World Action Model for Conveyor-Belt Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20735)
*Siyuan Ma,Yutian Zhang,Boshi Zhang,Qinglian Wu,Jiaqi Zhai,Dong Wei,Xiaojin Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ForeTime-VLA，一个将未来感知表示蒸馏到因果VLA策略中的密集pi0.5模型。通过Fast-WAM教师模型离线压缩当前与未来的视频潜在表示到白化的64维目标，在线使用8帧历史编码器预测该目标及操作阶段与归一化转移时间。在传送带抓取任务上，相比基线在MAE上提升2.63%，L2提升3.02%，真实机器人抓取成功率大幅超越pi0.5（44/90 vs 23/90），同时仅产生约2.5-2.9%的延迟开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 操控移动物体需要策略预先感知接触事件，但VLA策略通常仅从当前观测微调，缺乏未来预测能力。虽然世界动作模型（WAMs）可学习预测性动力学，但部署时运行视频规模教师或显式想象未来帧的成本过高，因此需要在保持推理因果性的同时获得未来感知能力。

Method: 核心方法是将未来的行动等价表示（从冻结Fast-WAM教师蒸馏）压缩为白化64维目标。离线阶段压缩当前与未来视频潜在表示；在线阶段用8帧历史编码器预测该目标、操作阶段和时间到转移。四个未来token和一个阶段token条件化VLM前缀，预测的未来与转移时间条件化动作专家。训练保留原始流匹配动作目标，并添加余弦、关系几何、阶段、时间到转移和行动等价目标。

Result: 在去重后的传送带数据集上，测试MAE从0.134119降至0.130593（2.63%提升），测试L2下降3.02%，延迟成本2.46-2.93%。真实机器人评估中，静止抓取成功率81.1%，慢速移动抓取58.9%，分别超出次优参考12.2和22.2个百分点。三种带速下完成44/90次抓取（pi0.5仅23/90），快速场景11/30对2/30。

Conclusion: 离线方向增益与真实机器人接触姿态失败减少的一致性表明，因果未来token蒸馏是提升动态操作能力的有效方式，且无需在部署时运行世界模型教师。

Abstract: Manipulating moving objects requires a policy to anticipate contact events, yet vision-language-action (VLA) policies are commonly fine-tuned from the current observation alone. World action models (WAMs) learn predictive dynamics, but running a video-scale teacher or explicitly imagining future frames at deployment is costly. We introduce ForeTime-VLA, a dense pi0.5 policy that distills a future-aware, action-equivalent representation from a frozen Fast-WAM-derived teacher while remaining causal at inference. Offline, current and future video latents are compressed into a whitened 64-D target. Online, an eight-frame history encoder predicts this target together with manipulation phase and normalized time-to-transition. Four future tokens and one phase token condition the VLM prefix, while the predicted future and transition horizon condition the action expert. Training retains the original flow-matching action target and adds cosine, relational geometry, phase, time-to-transition, and action-equivalence objectives. On a deduplicated conveyor-belt dataset, we compare 40k-step checkpoints on 768 matched windows per split. Test MAE decreases from 0.134119 to 0.130593 (2.63%; paired-bootstrap 95% CI: 0.82-4.48% improvement), and test L2 decreases by 3.02%, at a 2.46-2.93% latency cost. In quantitative real-robot evaluation, ForeTime-VLA achieves 81.1% stationary and 58.9% slow-moving grasp success, exceeding the next-best reference by 12.2 and 22.2 percentage points, respectively. Across three belt speeds, it completes 44/90 grasps versus 23/90 for pi0.5, including 11/30 versus 2/30 at fast speed. The agreement between offline orientation gains and reduced real-robot contact-pose failures supports causal future-token distillation as an effective way to improve dynamic manipulation without deploying the world-model teacher.

</details>


### [131] [Difficulty-Aware Semantic-ID Optimization for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20611)
*Xin Yu,Stephen Li,Sina Aghaei,Zifan Zhu,Jiamu Bai,Guanjie Huang,Bo Peng,Yiyao Liu,Lingzhou Xue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DASO（Difficulty-Aware Semantic-ID Optimization），一种针对语义ID生成式推荐系统的树感知后训练方法。DASO通过在线rollout分配策略，将每组rollout按前缀匹配深度进行画像，定位候选偏离目标路径的瓶颈SID层级，将部分rollout重新分配为前缀引导的补全，同时保留原始rollout用于对比，并引入SID前缀奖励和SFT锚点来解决vanilla GRPO在树结构任务中的失效问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义ID生成式推荐将检索和排序建模为层次化物品标识符的自回归生成。标准配方是SFT后接GRPO，但vanilla GRPO与这种树结构任务不匹配：在冻结的SFT检查点下，许多prompt的50-beam约束排序前16个候选中缺少精确目标，困难情况下甚至所有候选都不进入目标SID分支。当on-policy GRPO组同样缺少目标时，即使部分候选跟随目标路径的一部分，条目级奖励也可能产生微弱或退化的奖励变化。

Method: DASO将问题建模为在线rollout分配问题：(1) 对每个当前rollout组按前缀匹配深度进行画像，定位候选离开目标路径的瓶颈SID层级；(2) 将有界比例的组内rollout重新分配为前缀引导补全，同时保留原始rollout用于对比；(3) 引入SID前缀奖励以提供分级信用；(4) 使用辅助SFT锚点防止对SFT检查点已解决示例的回归。

Result: 在公开基准上，DASO在12项指标中的11项上优于MiniOneRec风格的GRPO，在12项指标中的9项上取得最佳结果；在内部推荐任务上，大多数层级级别的recall指标也得到提升。

Conclusion: DASO通过树感知的在线rollout分配和前缀引导训练，有效解决了vanilla GRPO在语义ID生成式推荐中因目标缺失导致的奖励退化问题，在公开和内部基准上均取得一致的性能提升。

Abstract: Semantic-ID-based generative recommendation casts retrieval and ranking as autoregressive generation over hierarchical item identifiers. A common recipe is SFT followed by GRPO, yet vanilla GRPO is poorly matched to this tree-structured task. Under the frozen SFT checkpoint, the exact target is absent from the first 16 candidates of the 50-beam constrained ranking for many prompts, and in harder cases none of these candidates enters the target SID branch. This prompt-level diagnostic motivates a training concern: when on-policy GRPO groups are similarly target-missing, item-level rewards may produce weak or degenerate reward variation even if some candidates follow part of the target path. We propose Difficulty-Aware Semantic-ID Optimization (DASO), a tree-aware post-training method that addresses this failure mode as an online rollout-allocation problem. Instead of using fixed difficulty buckets or uniformly injecting ground-truth completions, DASO profiles each current rollout group by prefix-match depth, locates the bottleneck SID levels where candidates leave the target path, and reallocates a bounded portion of the group to prefix-guided completions while retaining raw rollouts for contrast. A SID-prefix reward provides graded credit, while an auxiliary SFT anchor mitigates regression on examples already solved by the SFT checkpoint. On the public benchmarks, DASO improves over MiniOneRec-style GRPO on 11 of 12 metrics and achieves the best result on 9 of 12 metrics; it also improves most level-wise recall metrics on the internal recommendation task.

</details>


### [132] [Evaluating Skills, Not Just Agents: Agentic Continuous Evaluation of Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20614)
*Christopher Kevin,Narendran Raghavan,Jean-Francois Puget,Roshni Malani,Meghana Puvvadi,Moshe Abramovitch,Mohit Gupta,Rama Akkiraju,Subodh Prabhu,Yogesh Dangi,Wei Luo,Seong Hee Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ACES（Agentic Continuous Evaluation of Skills），一个仓库原生的技能评估框架，通过配对实时试验（有无目标技能）来评估Agent技能包的实际部署价值，使用ATIF标准化轨迹、六个默认运行时指标评分，并报告"技能提升度"（Skill Lift）。在145个真实企业技能、947个配对案例上的实验表明，ACES能捕获文档扫描无法观察的运行时信号（如技能执行、行为检查、技能效率），平均复合技能提升度为0.2134。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业Agent程序正从原型走向生产，可复用技能、工具和工作流包必须以证据而非文字描述来审查。现有门禁（扫描技能包的结构、风格、安全性）无法回答关键部署问题：能力包是否能在相同模型、沙盒和评分策略下帮助实时Agent完成企业任务。

Method: ACES框架包含四个核心要素：1）配对实时试验（有/无目标技能）；2）将轨迹规范化为Agent Trajectory Interchange Format (ATIF)；3）六个默认运行时指标评分；4）报告Skill Lift即目标技能对固定任务、框架、工作区和评分器的增加值。协议支持产品拥有的任务套件，可比较基线、技能、捆绑包、团队技能和插件目标。

Result: 在145个真实内部企业技能和公共目录技能上，扫描门禁测量的是互补方面（结构与LLM评判Spearman ρ=0.14，相关性弱）。在58/64个生产技能、四个主框架、947个评分配对案例中：平均复合Skill Lift为0.2134（95% CI [0.1967, 0.2301]）；平均仅结果提升为0.1799；复合提升在72.8%配对案例中为正。最大过程指标增益出现在技能执行、行为检查和技能效率，这些是文档扫描无法观察的信号（涉及发现、路由、工作流遵循和工具使用）。

Conclusion: ACES提供了一种仓库原生的、基于证据的技能评估方法，弥补了传统扫描门禁的不足——后者主要发现作者性问题但无法测量实际运行时价值。该方法已在NVIDIA SkillEvaluator中开源实现，用于评估技能和产品能力包的可部署性。

Abstract: Enterprise agent programs are moving from prototypes into production, where reusable skills, tools, and workflow packages must be reviewed with evidence rather than prose. Current gates often scan these artifacts for structure, style, and security, but they do not answer the deployment question: does the capability package help a live agent complete enterprise tasks under the same model, sandbox, and grading policy?
  We present ACES (Agentic Continuous Evaluation of Skills), a repository-native framework for evaluating skills and product capability packages as executable agent artifacts. ACES runs paired live trials with and without a target skill, normalizes trajectories into the Agent Trajectory Interchange Format (ATIF), grades six default runtime metrics, and reports Skill Lift: the target skill's added value for a fixed task, harness, workspace, and scorer. The same protocol supports product-owned task suites that compare baseline, skill, bundle, team-skill, and plugin targets.
  On 145 real skills from internal enterprise repositories and public catalogs, scan-only gates surface useful authoring issues but measure complementary facets (structural versus LLM-judge Spearman $ρ= 0.14$). Across 947 scored paired cases from 58 of 64 production skills and four primary harnesses, mean composite Skill Lift is 0.2134 (95\% paired-case CI [0.1967, 0.2301]); mean outcome-only lift, the average of accuracy and goal accuracy, is 0.1799. Composite lift is positive in 72.8\% of paired cases. The largest process-metric gains appear in skill execution, behavior check, and skill efficiency---signals about discovery, routing, workflow following, and tool use that document scans cannot observe. An open-source implementation of the methodology is available in NVIDIA SkillEvaluator.

</details>


### [133] [Dual-Cache Latent Space Communication between Heterogeneous Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20617)
*Jiyao Liu,Qi Zhang,Yaoyi Jia,Ziwen Kan,Song Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: XKV是一种用于多智能体LLM系统的潜在通信协议，通过训练一个轻量级翻译器，将共享者（Sharer）的KV缓存转换为接收者（Receiver）原生KV几何结构下的表示，实现了异构冻结模型间的上下文传递。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本通信将自回归解码置于关键路径且缺乏对接收者状态的感知；已有的潜在协议（C2C、LCF-X）存在三个限制：仅压缩共享者缓存、缺乏联合跨层记忆、假设层数与KV几何匹配。这些限制阻碍了异构LLM之间高效、灵活的通信。

Method: XKV包含三个核心组件：(1) 学习查询注意力池化两个缓存（共享者和接收者）；(2) 在接收者对齐的层token上进行自注意力，辅以学习到的层映射来协调不同网络深度，将池化摘要混合为紧凑的联合记忆；(3) 共享位置解码器，使接收者每个原始缓存位置通过逐头门控残差在原生KV几何中检索自己的表示。两个模型保持冻结，仅训练翻译器。

Result: 在45种数据集-模型对设置（6种异构和3种同模型配对，5个数据集）中，XKV获得最高宏分数和最佳平均排名。在ROPES上比LCF-X提升4.6个精确匹配点和4.2个F1点；在4/5的数据集上超越文本通信；训练参数减少76%；缓存对翻译速度快10.3倍（5.8ms vs 59.9ms）；端到端比LCF-X快26%，比文本通信快6.8倍。

Conclusion: XKV通过同时解决LCF-X的三个局限，实现了异构多智能体LLM系统间的高效潜在通信，在保持模型冻结的前提下以更少的训练参数、更快的推理速度取得了更优的性能，是潜在通信协议在该方向上的重要进展。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems split work across models, so answering often requires knowledge that sits in another agent's context: a Sharer has encoded information that a Receiver needs to complete its task. They usually communicate by exchanging text, which puts autoregressive decoding on the critical path and reduces the exchange to a discrete message written without sight of the receiver's state. Recent latent protocols instead translate the sharer's key-value (KV) cache into the receiver's: C2C supports heterogeneous models but requires both to read the same input, while LCF-X removes this shared-context requirement through position-free sharer-cache pooling.
